A trustworthy conversational agent should distinguish these two requests and respond in different epistemic registers: neutral attribution in the first case and stance expression in the second. Whether such a shift occurs-and whether it occurs coherently-is not directly assessed by existing benchmarks for accuracy, instruction following, or safety. We introduce ESFP, a behavioral benchmark that treats the contrast between externally attributed and self-attributed prompts as the fundamental unit of measurement. ESFP consists of 104 carefully controlled items spanning six epistemic categories and five phrasing templates, and evaluates model responses along four complementary dimensions: lexical self-attribution, representation-level responsiveness to role framing, sentence-level stance content density assessed by an LLM judge panel, and cross-condition stance consistency. Evaluating eight frontier models from five vendors, we find that epistemic flexibility is largely orthogonal to general model capability: a 27B open-weight model matches the strongest proprietary systems, the flagship model of one family underperforms its lightweight counterpart, and reasoning-optimized models do not consistently exhibit higher flexibility. Stance content density provides the strongest signal, while surface-level lexical markers such as 'I think' can change substantially without corresponding changes in expressed stance. We provide item-level bootstrap confidence intervals, weight-sensitivity analyses, and an explicit discussion of the interpretation limits of the composite score. ESFP measures a model's propensity to adapt its epistemic stance under changing attribution conditions, rather than a general competence measure.

</details>


### [36] [The One-Word Census: Answer-Choice Conformity Across 44 Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12796)
*Tapan Parikh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过“单词普查”这一极简工具，系统评估了44个语言模型在31个开放类别提示下的单词回答一致性，发现模型间高度收敛，但一致性程度随模型类型、代际及调优策略显著差异，且最新旗舰模型表现出异常高的一致性，甚至超过人类基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已证明语言模型输出存在收敛现象，但缺乏一种低成本、可复现、无需嵌入或人工评判的标准化量化工具，以揭示收敛的结构性规律及其与模型谱系、调优范式、代际演进的关系。

Method: 设计31个单轮提示（如“说一种树”），每模型重复4次，无系统提示；对44个模型收集答案，经标准化后精确匹配计算词频。定义“答案选择惊讶度”为模型答案在留一法汇总分布下的平均负对数概率，以此度量一致性。对比不同谱系、代际、调优类型模型，并与人类类别生成规范对比。

Result: 7/31类别中单一答案占比超80%，模型间一致性差异超4倍。人设/社区调优模型最发散，最新主线旗舰模型最一致（几乎无独有答案）。Claude、GPT、Qwen、Grok四大谱系内，一致性随代际上升，但最新旗舰Claude与GPT出现反转，提示顶层定位调整。排名对名单组成稳健（留一谱系法Spearman ρ=0.985）。20个共享类别中18个模型分布比人类更集中。

Conclusion: 单词普查揭示了语言模型输出收敛的精细结构：一致性非单调随规模增强，受调优目标与市场定位强烈调节；最新旗舰模型的高度一致性可能反映训练数据污染、RLHF偏好对齐或商业策略趋同，值得持续监测。

Abstract: When a language model must pick one answer from a large space of equally valid options, which does it pick -- and how often is it the same answer every other model picks? Asked to "pick a word -- any word," 44 models chose "serendipity" 41% of the time. We characterize this convergence with a deliberately minimal instrument: 31 single-turn prompts, each naming a category with many valid one-word answers ("Name a tree."), asked four times per model with no system prompt. Analysis is exact-match on normalized tokens -- no embeddings, no judge -- at about a dollar per model. That models converge is well documented; our contribution is the instrument itself -- the One-Word Census -- and what it reveals about the structure of the convergence. We score each model by answer-choice surprisal: the average $-\log2$ probability of its answers under the pooled answers of all other models, leave-one-out. Convergence is extreme -- in 7 of 31 categories one answer takes over 80% of all answers -- yet conformity varies more than fourfold across models, and the variation is structured. Persona- and community-tuned models are the most divergent; the newest mainline flagships are the most conformist, producing almost no answer no other model gave. Within four lineages (Claude, GPT, Qwen, Grok) conformity rises with each generation -- but reverses for the latest flagship Claude and GPT models, a possible early signal of repositioning at the top tier. Rankings are robust to roster composition (leave-one-family-out rho = 0.985). Against human category-production norms, the field is more concentrated than people in 18 of 20 shared categories. All prompts, transcripts, and code are public.

</details>


### [37] [Knowledgeless Language Models: Suppressing Parametric Recall for Evidence-Grounded Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12831)
*Roi Cohen,Yvan Carré,Nick Lechtenbörger,Hendrik Droste,Lucas Kerschke,Russa Biswas,Gerard de Melo,Jan Buys*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出知识匿名化预训练范式（KLLM），通过在预训练语料中匿名化命名实体来移除实体链接的事实监督信号，系统性地将模型从参数化记忆转向证据驱动推理。实验表明，KLLM 在保留语言能力的同时显著降低闭卷事实回忆，却在上下文问答、事实核查、幻觉检测等任务上持续优于同规模基线，尤其在不完美检索证据下表现出更强鲁棒性（相对提升 20–25%），并具备更好的校准指标（ECE、Brier score、AUROC）与拒答可靠性。结论：预训练阶段抑制实体监督可诱导模型的认识论行为转变，为检索增强系统提供更敏感的基座模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在参数中编码大量事实知识，导致知识过时、不完整或与上下文冲突时出现不可靠行为（幻觉、拒答失败等）。现有缓解手段多集中在推理阶段（RAG、工具调用），缺乏从预训练源头控制知识获取的系统性研究。本文旨在探究：通过修改预训练信号，能否从根本上将模型的认识倾向从参数化回忆转向证据依赖，从而在真实检索场景下提升可靠性。

Method: 提出 Knowledge-Less Language Models (KLLM) 预训练范式：在大规模预训练语料中系统性地匿名化所有命名实体（人名、地名、组织名等），切断实体与事实知识的显式关联，从而移除实体链接的事实监督信号。保留语言结构、句法及非实体语义，使用与标准 LLM 相同的架构、规模（1B–7B）及训练预算进行对比实验。评估维度涵盖闭卷事实回忆、上下文问答、事实核查、幻觉检测、不完美检索鲁棒性、校准指标（ECE、Brier score、AUROC）及拒答行为。

Result: KLLM 在多个模型规模上均表现出：(1) 闭卷事实回忆大幅下降；(2) 上下文问答、事实核查、幻觉检测任务持续超越同规模基线；(3) 在含噪/不完美检索证据的 RAG 场景中获得 20–25% 相对性能提升；(4) 校准指标全面改善（ECE、Brier score、AUROC 均优于基线）；(5) 拒答行为更可靠，能在证据不足时主动拒答而非幻觉生成。

Conclusion: 预训练阶段抑制实体链接监督可根本性地改变模型的认识论行为：KLLM 减少对参数化知识的依赖，增强对外部证据的敏感性，从而在真实检索增强场景下实现更高的可靠性与鲁棒性。这表明预训练时的知识获取控制可作为 RAG 与工具调用系统的正交补充，为构建可信赖的语言模型提供新范式。

Abstract: Language models encode substantial factual knowledge in their parameters, which can lead to unreliable behavior when this knowledge is outdated, incomplete, or misaligned with the provided context. In this work, we study whether modifying the pretraining signal can systematically shift models away from parametric recall and toward evidence-grounded reasoning. We introduce Knowledge--''Less'' Language Models (KLLMs), a fundamentally different epistemic training paradigm for LLMs, which are pretrained on corpora in which named entities are anonymized, thereby removing a primary channel for entity-linked factual supervision. This intervention substantially reduces closed-book factual recall, while often improving performance on tasks where relevant information is provided as context. Across multiple model scales, KLLMs consistently outperform matched baselines on contextual question answering, fact verification, and hallucination detection benchmarks. Crucially, in retrieval-grounded settings with imperfect evidence, KLLMs show improved robustness and achieve up to 20--25\% relative gains over standard language models. They further exhibit better calibration, with improved ECE, Brier score, and AUROC, as well as more reliable abstention behavior. Our results demonstrate that suppressing entity-linked supervision during pretraining induces a shift in epistemic behavior: KLLMs rely less on parametric knowledge and more on external evidence, leading to improved reliability under realistic conditions. This suggests that pretraining-time control over knowledge acquisition can complement retrieval-augmented and tool-based systems by providing a more evidence-sensitive base model.

</details>


### [38] [Can LLMs Write Reliable Rubrics? A Meta-Evaluation for Experiment Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12835)
*Hanhua Hong,Yizhi Li,Jiaoyan Chen,Luu Gia Huy,Sophia Ananiadou,Jung-jae Kim,Chenghua Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地对用于论文复现评估的LLM生成评分表进行元评估，将评分表重构为清单式格式，在两个基座模型上测试四种生成设置，通过语义相似度（内在）与评分对齐（外在）两个维度评估。结果显示增强设置显著提升下游评估对齐度，最强设置逼近人类基线，但内在提升有限；进一步分析揭示LLM生成评分表过于细粒度、倾向高分、对论文领域适应性较差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于评分表的评估是评价LLM研究代理开放式输出的有前景方法，尤其在论文复现任务中，直接论文到代码库比较易产生幻觉。然而构建论文特定评分表需要大量专家工作，限制了PaperBench等基准的可扩展性。因此需要系统评估LLM自动生成评分表的质量与可行性。

Method: 将评分表重构为清单式格式；在两个基座模型上评估四种生成设置；采用内在评估（语义相似度）与外在评估（与真实评分表的评分对齐）相结合的元评估框架；并进行定性分析以揭示生成评分表的特征模式。

Result: 增强设置显著提升下游评估对齐度，最强设置性能逼近人类基线；内在指标（语义相似度）提升幅度较小；LLM生成评分表普遍存在过度细粒度、高分偏见、跨领域适应性不足等问题。

Conclusion: LLM生成评分表在论文复现评估中具有实用潜力，增强生成策略可使其接近人工评分表水平，但仍需解决粒度控制、评分校准及领域适应性等局限性，以实现大规模可扩展的自动化评估基准。

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluation is a promising approach for assessing open-ended outputs from LLM-based research agents, particularly in paper reproduction, where direct paper-to-repository comparison is prone to hallucination. However, constructing paper-specific rubrics requires substantial expert effort, limiting the scalability of benchmarks such as PaperBench. In this work, we present, to our knowledge, the first systematic meta-evaluation of LLM-generated rubrics for paper reproduction. We reformulate rubrics into a checklist-style format and evaluate four generation settings across two backbone models. We meta-evaluate generated rubrics intrinsically by semantic similarity and extrinsically by score alignment with ground-truth rubrics. Our results show that the augmented settings substantially improves downstream evaluation alignment, with the strongest setting approaching the human baseline, while intrinsic gains are more modest. Further analyses reveal that LLM-generated rubrics are often overly fine-grained, biased toward high scores, and less adaptive to paper domains, highlighting both the affordances and limitations.

</details>


### [39] [Evaluating Large Language Models on Misconceptions in Multi-Turn Medical Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12884)
*Monica Munnangi,Saiph Savage*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个多轮医疗对话数据集ThReadMed-QA，评估大语言模型在多轮对话中检测和纠正患者误解的能力，发现即使是先进模型在后续轮次中性能显著下降，主要由于错误传播，且即使在正确上下文下表现也不完美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估框架未捕捉大语言模型在多轮医疗对话中处理患者错误预设和误解的能力，而这类交互在真实医疗咨询中常见且对安全至关重要。

Method: 构建了包含2,437个真实患者-医生对话线程（8,204个问答对）的ThReadMed-QA数据集，采用基于评分标准的LLM-as-a-Judge框架评估五个大语言模型在多轮对话中识别和纠正误解的能力，并通过将模型历史输出替换为医生回复的神谕分析来量化错误传播影响。

Result: 前沿模型（如GPT-5和Claude-Haiku）在初始问题上纠正误解的准确率约85%，但在两轮追问后下降至约50%。神谕分析显示性能下降主要源于错误传播，即使在正确上下文下模型表现仍不完美。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在多轮医疗对话中纠正误解的能力随轮次显著衰减，导致在面向患者场景中提供不一致且潜在不安全的指导，凸显了需要能捕捉多轮行为的评估框架。

Abstract: Patients seeking medical information often ask questions that embed incorrect assumptions or misconceptions. In such cases, safe medical communication requires not only answering the question, but identifying and correcting the underlying false belief. These interactions naturally unfold over multiple turns, a pattern now mirrored in interactions with LLMs. Yet current evaluation frameworks do not capture model behavior in these settings, where misconceptions can emerge, persist, or evolve over the course of a conversation. Whether LLMs can reliably correct such misconceptions over time remains largely unexamined. To study this, we introduce ThReadMed-QA, a multi-turn medical dialogue dataset of 2,437 patient-physician conversation threads comprising 8,204 question-answer pairs, derived from real patient interactions on AskDocs. This dataset enables systematic evaluation of whether models can detect and correct misconceptions under a multi-turn context. We evaluate five LLMs using a rubric-based LLM-as-a-Judge framework that scores responses based on their ability to identify and correct misconceptions. Our experiments reveal a consistent pattern: even frontier models that can address misconceptions in a single interaction degrade substantially over subsequent turns. GPT-5 and Claude-Haiku correct these false presuppositions around 85% on initial questions but drop to roughly 50% within two follow-ups. An oracle analysis replacing prior model outputs with physician responses shows that much of the degradation is driven by error propagation, while performance remains imperfect even under correct context. Even when models tend to correct misconceptions initially, their performance degrades substantially over later turns, leading to inconsistent and potentially unsafe guidance in patient-facing settings and highlighting the need for evaluation frameworks that capture multi-turn behavior.

</details>


### [40] [LLM Judges Can Be Too Generous When There Is No Reference Answer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12885)
*Chalamalasetti Kranti,Sowmya Vajjala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究无参考设置下LLM裁判评估开放式模型响应的可靠性，通过校准实验和敏感性实验发现：裁判倾向于在无参考答案时过度肯定错误回答，加入参考答案后裁判决策翻转率高达85%，且变化与人工标注一致。结论强调需在无参考评估前用参考感知方式校准裁判，并提供通用校准蓝图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM裁判在无参考评估中的广泛应用，其可靠性尚不明确。本文旨在系统考察裁判对任务的知识掌握程度以及参考答案在提示词中的存在与位置如何影响裁判判断，填补该领域的实证空白。

Method: 采用两阶段管道：(a) 校准实验，测量裁判模型对被评估任务的内在知识；(b) 敏感性实验，在提示词中引入参考答案并变换其位置，观察裁判正确/错误决策的翻转情况。实验覆盖三种语言，并与部分人工标注对比验证。

Result: 裁判模型在无参考答案时倾向于过度给分；加入参考答案后，裁判决策在部分设置中翻转高达85%，且这些参考驱动的变化与人工判断基本一致。

Conclusion: 在无参考评估场景中直接使用LLM裁判存在显著风险，必须先通过参考感知的校准样本对裁判进行校准。本文提出的方法论为研究者和实践者提供了可复用的裁判校准蓝图。

Abstract: LLM judges are increasingly being used to evaluate open-ended model responses, often in no-reference settings where a ground-truth answer is unavailable. However, can they reliably assess in such evaluation setups? We explore this question in this paper through a two stage pipeline with a) calibration experiments that assess the judge model's knowledge of the task it is evaluating, and b) sensitivity experiments that assess how the judge model's performance is impacted by the presence and positioning of the reference answer in the prompt. Across experiments covering three languages, we show that the judge models we evaluated tend to over-credit incorrect answers in the absence of a reference answer, and adding reference answer information to the prompt flips the judge model's correct/incorrect decisions by as much as 85% in some experimental settings. Comparison with a subset of human annotations shows that these reference-driven changes generally align with human judgments. Our results emphasize the need for calibrating the LLM judges with a sample with reference-aware evaluation before using them in reference-free setups reliably, and our methodology provides a blueprint for researchers and practitioners in doing such calibration of LLM judges for other tasks.

</details>


### [41] [The Illusion of Robustness: Aggregate Accuracy Hides Prediction Flips under Task-Irrelevant Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12963)
*Yanzhe Zhang,Sanmi Koyejo,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现大语言模型在整体准确率上对无关上下文表现稳健，但单样本层面存在显著不稳定性：甚至无意义伪词也能翻转少量样本预测，且受影响样本具有模型特异性。不稳定性受上下文类型、长度、推理算力及模型开发阶段调节，揭示了被整体指标掩盖的尾部风险，呼吁开展单样本可靠性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大模型部署于长上下文场景，任务输入常混杂无关信息。现有评估多关注整体准确率，忽略了单样本层面的鲁棒性风险。本文旨在系统量化无关上下文对单样本预测的扰动效应，揭示聚合指标掩盖的尾部风险。

Method: 在受控实验中，向基准问题前拼接任务无关上下文（包括语义无意义的随机字符组合伪词），跨多模型、多数据集测量单样本预测翻转率；系统变换上下文类型、长度、测试时计算量及模型开发阶段，分析不稳定性的调节因子。

Result: 整体准确率变化微小，但单样本层面出现双向翻转：部分样本性能下降、部分提升。伪词也能诱导显著翻转，受影响样本高度模型特异化。不稳定性随上下文长度增加而上升，随推理算力增加而下降，且在模型训练后期减弱。

Conclusion: 聚合准确率掩盖了上下文诱导的单样本尾部风险。大模型对无关上下文的鲁棒性在单样本层面脆弱且模型特异，需建立单样本可靠性评估范式，并关注上下文长度、推理算力等调节因子对鲁棒性的影响。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) grow more capable, they are increasingly deployed in context-rich settings where task inputs are often accompanied by long, partially irrelevant context. In a controlled setting, we find that state-of-the-art models often appear robust to task-irrelevant context at the aggregate level: prepending it to benchmark questions causes little change in overall accuracy. This aggregate stability, however, masks significant per-example instability. Even semantically meaningless pseudo-words, formed by randomly combining characters, can markedly shift model predictions on a small fraction of examples, degrading performance on some while improving it on others. This two-sided effect holds consistently across a wide range of models and datasets, yet the affected examples are largely model-specific. We further show that this instability is modulated by context type, context length, test-time compute, and model development stage. Together, our findings reveal context-induced tail risks concealed by aggregate accuracy, motivating per-example reliability evaluation of language models.

</details>


### [42] [PalmClaw: A Native On-Device Agent Framework for Mobile Phones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13027)
*Hongru Cai,Yongqi Li,Ran Wei,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PalmClaw 是一个原生运行在移动设备上的开源智能体框架，通过将设备能力封装为显式工具，显著提升任务成功率和执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动智能体主要通过 GUI 操作（点击、滑动、输入）控制智能手机，形成冗长且依赖界面的序列，无法直接访问设备能力，且执行边界难以定义。需要一种原生运行于移动设备、能直接调用设备能力且具有明确执行边界的框架。

Method: 提出 PalmClaw 框架，在移动设备上原生运行，管理会话、记忆、技能、工具和智能体循环。将设备能力暴露为具有显式参数、结构化结果和明确执行边界的设备工具，使智能体能直接使用移动能力，同时保持每个动作显式且可控。

Result: 实验表明，相比最强基线，任务成功率相对提升 11.5%，完成时间减少 94.9%，设置负担更低，并展示了执行边界如何应用的追踪。

Conclusion: PalmClaw 通过原生设备工具抽象和明确的执行边界，解决了移动智能体依赖 GUI 操作、执行边界模糊的问题，显著提升了任务完成率和效率，为移动端智能体部署提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have moved beyond generating responses to executing multi-step tasks by calling tools, observing the results, and iteratively deciding the next action. Most agent systems run on desktops or servers, which support tool use and task automation. Mobile devices are also important agent environments because they are widely accessible and contain users' data, sensors, and daily-use applications. Existing mobile agents mainly operate smartphones through graphical user interface (GUI) actions such as tapping, swiping, and typing, which often form long, interface-dependent sequences, cannot directly access device capabilities, and make execution boundaries difficult to define. We present \textbf{PalmClaw}, an open-source agent framework that runs natively on mobile phones and manages the sessions, memory, skills, tools, and agent loop directly on the device. PalmClaw exposes device capabilities as device tools with explicit arguments, structured results, and clearly defined execution boundaries. This design enables agents to use mobile capabilities directly while keeping each action explicit and controlled. Experiments show an 11.5\% relative improvement in task success and a 94.9\% reduction in completion time over the strongest baseline, with lower setup burden and traces illustrating how execution boundaries are applied. Code is available at https://github.com/ModalityDance/PalmClaw.

</details>


### [43] [Scaling Point-in-Time Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11889)
*Bryan Kelly,Semyon Malamud,Johannes Schwab,Teng Andrea Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文展示了通过大规模训练，时间点语言模型（仅使用特定日期前的数据训练）可以显著缩小与无时间限制模型的性能差距，并发布了完整的可复现流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无限制互联网语料上训练的大语言模型不可避免地包含未来信息，导致前瞻偏差，破坏了金融和社会科学中回测和因果推断的有效性。时间点模型虽能消除这种泄露，但通常性能远落后于无约束模型。

Method: 在FineWeb的1万亿按时间顺序过滤的token上，训练高达40亿参数的仅解码器Transformer，构建覆盖2013-2024年的月度模型检查点序列；并通过LoRA进行指令微调以提升下游可用性。

Result: 在常识推理和语言理解基准上，模型性能接近同规模领先开放权重模型（如Gemma-3-4B和LLaMA-7B），尽管在部分任务上仍存差距；LoRA微调进一步改善了下游任务表现。

Conclusion: 规模扩大能显著缩小时间点模型与无约束模型的性能鸿沟。发布的完整流水线（数据集构建、训练基础设施、评测代码）支持可复现的时间点语言建模，服务于需要严格时间有效性的研究应用。

Abstract: Large language models trained on unrestricted internet corpora inevitably embed information from the future, introducing lookahead bias that compromises the validity of backtests and causal inference in finance and the social sciences. Point-in-time language models--trained exclusively on text available up to each calendar date--eliminate this leakage by construction, but existing efforts typically produce models that lag substantially behind their unconstrained counterparts. We show that this performance gap can be substantially narrowed through scale. Training decoder-only transformers with up to 4 billion parameters on 1 trillion chronologically filtered tokens from FineWeb, we construct a sequence of monthly model checkpoints spanning 2013-2024. Across a range of common-sense reasoning and language understanding benchmarks, our models approach the performance of leading open-weight models of comparable size (e.g., Gemma-3-4B and LLaMA-7B) trained on temporally unrestricted data, although a performance gap remains on several tasks. Instruction fine-tuning via LoRA further improves downstream usability. We release the complete pipeline--including dataset construction, training infrastructure, and evaluation code--to enable reproducible point-in-time language modeling and to support research applications that require strict temporal validity.

</details>


### [44] [CANDI: Contextual Alignment for Niche Domains Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11891)
*Megha Chakraborty,Darssan L. Eswaramoorthi,Het Riteshkumar Shah,Madhur Thareja,Michelle A Ihetu,Harshul Raj Surana,Kaushik Roy,Amit Sheth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出CANDI-QA数据集，用于评估大语言模型在医疗诊断、金融咨询等专业领域的上下文对齐能力，包含信息辅助型和应用推理型两类问题，并提供MTSS-Net神经符号基线模型，实验揭示现有LLMs在高风险领域的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有问答基准难以捕捉专业领域所需的细微上下文落地、用户感知和领域理解，亟需专门评估LLMs在专业场景下提供准确、上下文敏感且用户对齐答案的能力。

Method: 构建CANDI-QA数据集，包含专家策划的两类问答对：1) 信息辅助问题（直接事实查询），2) 应用推理问题（多跳推理需情境推断）。评估十余个从开源到专有的多样化语言模型。提出MTSS-Net基线，结合神经检索与规则推理的轻量级神经符号框架。

Result: 实验显示当前LLMs在专业领域实现上下文对齐面临深层挑战，缺乏增强上下文或符号集成时表现受限。MTSS-Net作为基线展示了神经符号融合的潜力。

Conclusion: CANDI-QA为推进上下文感知语言模型研究提供关键基准，能激发开发适用于高风险领域的稳健、可信AI系统。

Abstract: The deployment of large language models (LLMs) in specialized domains like medical diagnostics and financial advisory necessitates evaluating capabilities beyond general knowledge. Traditional question-answering benchmarks often fail to capture the nuanced contextual grounding, user awareness, and domain understanding these fields require. To address this, we introduce CANDI-QA (Contextual Alignment for Niche Domains Question Answering), a novel dataset evaluating LLMs on delivering accurate, context-sensitive, and user-aligned answers in specialized settings. CANDI-QA features expert-curated question-answer pairs structured into two categories: (1) Information Assistance Questions, which are direct, factual queries requiring precise extraction, and (2) Applied Inference Questions, which are multi-hop reasoning tasks needing situational inference to generate actionable insights. We evaluate over ten diverse language models, from compact open-source to state-of-the-art proprietary systems. As a robust baseline, we present MTSS-Net, a lightweight neuro-symbolic framework combining neural retrieval with rule-based reasoning. Our findings highlight the profound challenges of achieving contextual alignment in niche domains, revealing the limitations of current LLMs without enhanced contextual or symbolic integration. Ultimately, CANDI-QA serves as a critical benchmark for advancing research in context-aware language models, stimulating the development of robust, trustworthy AI for high-stakes domains.

</details>


### [45] [G-SHARE: A Guideline-Based Structured Reasoning Framework for Human-Factor Event Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11892)
*Xingyu Xiao,Mao Du,Jiejuan Tong,Jingang Liang,Haitao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出G-SHARE框架，将CNNP九步人因事件诊断指南转化为多阶段诊断流水线，包括证据提取、逐步诊断推理和事后一致性修复，在真实核电站人因事件报告数据集上显著优于单次提示和传统机器学习基线，验证了将专家诊断指南转化为可审计推理工作流的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核电站人因事件诊断对运行经验反馈至关重要，但诊断质量高度依赖专家对叙事报告的解读和基于指南的推理。现有数据驱动或单次大语言模型方法缺乏结构化推理、与正式诊断指南对齐有限，且可能产生逻辑不一致的结论。

Method: 提出G-SHARE框架，将CNNP九步人因事件诊断指南运营化为多阶段诊断流水线：证据提取、逐步诊断推理、事后一致性修复。构建了来自中国核工业来源的真实人因事件报告数据集，并由领域专家标注金标准子集用于评估。

Result: G-SHARE大幅超越单次提示和传统机器学习基线，最强版本达到最佳整体准确率和宏F1。消融实验表明结构化推理和一致性强制对鲁棒诊断至关重要，特别是在弱提示条件下。

Conclusion: 研究证明将专家诊断指南转化为可审计推理工作流具有重要价值，为安全关键行业的智能人因分析提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Human-factor event diagnosis is essential for learning from operational events in nuclear power plants, yet its quality depends strongly on expert interpretation of narrative reports and guideline-based reasoning.Existing data-driven or one-shot large language model approaches often lack structured reasoning, have limited alignment with formal diagnostic guidelines, and may generate logically inconsistent conclusions. To address this issue, this study proposes G-SHARE, a guideline-based structured reasoning framework that operationalizes the CNNP nine-step human-factor event diagnosis guideline into a multi-stage diagnostic pipeline.The framework consists of evidence extraction, stepwise diagnostic reasoning, and post-hoc consistency repair, enabling explicit use of report evidence, intermediate rationale generation, and logical validation of diagnostic outputs. A dataset of real human-factor event reports was constructed from Chinese nuclear industry sources, and a gold-standard subset annotated by domain experts was used for evaluation. Results show that G-SHARE substantially outperforms one-shot prompting and traditional machine learning baselines, with the strongest version achieving the best overall accuracy and macro-F1. Ablation results further indicate that structured reasoning and consistency enforcement are critical to robust diagnosis, especially under weak prompting conditions. The findings demonstrate the value of transforming expert diagnostic guidelines into auditable reasoning workflows, providing a practical pathway for intelligent human-factor analysis in safety-critical industries.

</details>


### [46] [Graph-Based Detection of Disinformation Narrative Diffusion between Russian and Ukrainian Telegram Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11894)
*Yuliia Vistak,Viktoriia Makovska,Vera Schmitt,Veronika Solopova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于图的框架，结合弱监督与传播图分析，在Telegram生态系统中检测和分析虚假信息叙事，通过聚类语义相关声明为叙事级簇并建模其跨频道扩散，实现协同叙事放大的检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上虚假信息叙事因放大规模大、演化快、语言变异性强而难以检测；现有基于帖子级的分析无法捕捉协同叙事放大现象。

Method: 基于图的框架，结合弱监督学习与传播图分析；将语义相关的声明聚合为叙事级簇，建模其在互联频道网络中的扩散过程；整合文本信号与网络结构特征。

Result: 实验结果表明，整合文本信号与网络结构提供了一种可扩展的虚假信息叙事检测方法，并能揭示其在大规模即时通讯环境中的传播机制。

Conclusion: 该图方法有效解决了虚假信息叙事检测中的规模、演化和语言变异挑战，为理解协同叙事放大提供了新视角和工具。

Abstract: Detecting disinformation narratives on social media is challenging due to the scale of amplification, rapid evolution, and linguistic variability of online content. We propose a graph-based framework for identifying and analyzing disinformation narratives in Telegram ecosystems by combining weak supervision with propagation graph analysis. The approach aggregates semantically related claims into narrative-level clusters and models their diffusion across interconnected channels. This enables the detection of coordinated narrative amplification that is difficult to capture through post-level analysis alone. Our results demonstrate that integrating textual signals with network structure provides a scalable method for detecting disinformation narratives and offers insights into how they propagate within large-scale messaging environments.

</details>

### [47] [Repairing Shape-Prior Shortcuts in Long-Range Single-Shot Fringe Projection Profilometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11928)
*Adam Haroon,Cody Fleming,Beiwen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对单次拍摄条纹投影轮廓测量（FPP）网络存在的“形状先验捷径”问题，提出PhiCalNet架构：网络输出包裹相位(sinφ, cosφ)，再经固定可微校准层映射为深度，并引入条纹级次作为辅助输入，从架构层面消除捷径。实验表明PhiCalNet将物体平均绝对误差（MAE）从14.54 mm降至4.46 mm（3.3倍提升），残差仅集中在0.103%的±π相位包裹断点；三帧扩展可达1.16 mm。可解释性分析与像素级一致性不确定性量化（FPP首创）均定位误差于同一断点，拒绝顶5%高不确定度像素可使RMSE降低64%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单帧FPP网络直接回归深度，优化器易陷入“形状先验捷径”：从物体边界而非条纹相位恢复深度。在光真实合成基准上，UNet基线MAE停滞在14.54 mm，增加数据量或模型容量均无法突破，因为假设空间未变。需从架构层面强制网络学习相位-深度物理映射，而非依赖损失惩罚。

Method: 1. PhiCalNet架构：网络输出包裹相位的正弦/余弦表示(sinφ, cosφ)，再通过固定可微分的校准层（编码相机-投影仪几何）映射为绝对深度，从架构上切断形状先验捷径。2. 引入条纹级次作为辅助输入，解决单帧映射非单射问题；灵敏度分析证实其对真实解码误差具鲁棒性。3. 对比实验：物理信息神经网络（PINN）以软约束形式引入相同物理先验，结果无提升，确认架构选择为关键因素。4. 可解释性分析与像素级一致性不确定性量化（快照不一致性）双重验证误差定位。

Result: 1. 单帧PhiCalNet物体MAE从14.54 mm降至4.46 mm（3.3倍），残差99.9%集中在±π相位包裹断点（占像素0.103%）。2. 三帧扩展MAE达1.16 mm。3. 相位为最可解码的内部特征，证实网络学到了物理映射。4. 首创FPP像素级一致性不确定性量化，不确定性高区域与相位断点精确重合；拒绝顶5%高不确定度像素，RMSE下降64%，而基线仅3.5%。

Conclusion: PhiCalNet通过显式相位输出与固定校准层的架构设计，成功消除了单帧FPP网络的形状先验捷径，大幅提升测量精度并将残差局限于相位包裹断点。引入条纹级次辅助输入有效解决了单射性问题。可解释性与不确定性量化双重验证了误差来源，并展示了基于不确定性的像素剔除策略显著降低RMSE的潜力，为高可靠单帧三维轮廓测量提供了新范式。

Abstract: Single-shot fringe projection profilometry (FPP) networks that regress depth directly can exploit a shape-prior shortcut, recovering depth from object boundaries rather than from fringe phase. On a photorealistic synthetic benchmark (15,600 fringe images, 50 objects at 1.5-2.1 m standoff), the best such UNet baseline plateaus at 14.54 mm object mean absolute error (MAE), and neither more data nor more capacity removes the shortcut, because neither changes the hypothesis space the optimizer searches. We introduce PhiCalNet, which outputs a wrapped-phase representation $(\sinφ, \cosφ)$ and maps it to depth through a fixed differentiable calibration layer, removing the shape-prior solution architecturally rather than by a loss penalty. Because the single-shot mapping is non-injective without fringe order, PhiCalNet takes the fringe order as auxiliary input, an assumption a sensitivity analysis shows tolerates realistic decoding error; a physics-informed (PINN) baseline with the same physics as a soft penalty yields no gain, isolating the architectural choice as the operative factor. PhiCalNet reduces object MAE 3.3x to 4.46 mm, its residual confined to 0.103% of pixels at the $\pmπ$ wrap discontinuity, and a three-frame extension reaches 1.16 mm. Two checks agree: interpretability makes phase the most decodable internal feature, and pixel-wise conformal uncertainty quantification, to our knowledge the first for FPP, localizes error at the same discontinuity, where rejecting the top 5% of pixels by snapshot disagreement cuts root-mean-square error by 64% versus 3.5% for the baseline.

</details>


### [48] [Qubit-Efficient Quantum Search for Hyperdimensional Decomposition via Logarithmic Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11936)
*Sanggeon Yun,Hyunwoo Oh,Ryozo Masukawa,Raheeb Hassan,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子比特高效的超维计算（HDC）分解框架，将超向量表示的量子比特成本从O(D)降至O(log D)，同时保持O(√(N^F))的二次搜索优势，实验显示量子比特用量最多减少2000倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子HDC分解方法虽提供二次搜索加速，但依赖O(D)量子比特的超向量显式编码，导致量子比特资源需求随维度线性增长，难以大规模部署。

Method: 引入对数超向量编码与绑定编码，设计可逆超向量查找算子实现稠密超向量的电路级操控，结合改进的Dürr-Høyer搜索过程，在保持O(√(N^F))搜索复杂度的同时大幅压缩量子比特开销。

Result: 实验验证了相似度计算正确性、可执行规模下的精准分解能力，相比基于显式D量子比特编码的基线方法，量子比特缩放显著改善，最高实现2000倍量子比特减少。

Conclusion: 该框架在保留量子搜索加速优势的前提下，通过对数编码与可逆算子实现了量子比特资源的指数级节省，为大规模HDC分解任务的量子实现提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Hyperdimensional Computing (HDC) represents symbols using high-dimensional hypervectors of dimension $D$. In hypervector decomposition, the objective is to recover $F$ constituent hypervectors, each drawn from a codebook of size $N$, from a bound target hypervector. This requires searching over $N^F$ candidate tuples, making the task computationally prohibitive at scale. Recent quantum approach provides a quadratic search advantage, but typically rely on qubit-inefficient $O(D)$-qubit hypervector representations. We propose a qubit-efficient quantum framework for HDC decomposition that reduces the representation cost to $O(\log D)$. The framework introduces logarithmic hypervector and binding encodings, together with a reversible hypervector lookup operator for circuit-level manipulation of dense hypervectors. Combined with a modified Dürr-Høyer search procedure, the method preserves $O(\sqrt{N^F})$ search complexity while substantially reducing qubit usage. Experimental results validate correct similarity computation, accurate decomposition in executable regimes, and significantly improved qubit scaling over baselines based on explicit $D$-qubit hypervector encodings, achieving up to $2{,}000\times$ fewer qubits.

</details>


### [49] [CARE-LoRA: Compressed Activation REconstruction for Memory-Efficient LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11940)
*Gengyu Zhang,Haiyin Ran,Zhengbao He,Yuhang Liu,Hanling Tian,Zhehao Huang,Xiaolin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARE-LoRA利用LoRA的低秩投影结构，用压缩激活替代全量输入激活，并通过轻量级重构矩阵在反向传播中恢复梯度信号，显著降低微调内存占用同时保持或超越标准LoRA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大规模预训练模型参数量持续增长，在有限内存预算下进行微调愈发困难。LoRA虽大幅减少可训练参数，但为反向传播保留的激活值成为主要内存瓶颈。

Method: 提出CARE-LoRA框架：1) 利用LoRA分支天然产生的低秩压缩激活替代全量输入激活；2) 前向传播中计算轻量级重构矩阵（额外计算开销可忽略）；3) 反向传播时用重构矩阵恢复梯度信号，保持LoRA矩阵完全可训练。

Result: 在多种模型和下游任务上的大量实验表明，CARE-LoRA在大幅降低整体内存占用的同时，达到与标准LoRA及代表性LoRA变体相当甚至更优的性能。

Conclusion: CARE-LoRA通过数据感知的压缩激活重构机制，有效解决了LoRA微调中的激活内存瓶颈，实现了无性能损失的内存高效微调。

Abstract: As the scale of large pre-trained models continues to grow, fine-tuning them under limited memory budgets has become increasingly challenging. Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), currently one of the most widely adopted parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, mitigates this challenge by optimizing only low-rank adaptation matrices, thereby greatly reducing the number of trainable parameters. With the parameter overhead substantially reduced, the activations retained for backpropagation have emerged as the primary remaining memory bottleneck during LoRA fine-tuning. To address this, we propose CARE-LoRA, a data-aware Compressed Activation REconstruction framework. By exploiting the inherent projection structure of LoRA, CARE-LoRA replaces the full input activation with the low-rank compressed activation naturally produced by the LoRA branch. It further computes a lightweight reconstruction matrix during the forward pass with negligible additional computation cost, which is used during backpropagation to reconstruct the gradient signal, thereby keeping LoRA matrices fully trainable. Extensive experiments across diverse models and downstream tasks demonstrate that, while substantially reducing the overall memory footprint, CARE-LoRA achieves competitive or even superior performance compared with standard LoRA and representative LoRA variants. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/fishandyu/CARE-LoRA .

</details>


### [50] [How Query Visibility Changes KV-Cache Compression Rankings: A Matched-Budget Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11942)
*Daming Luo,Christy Liang,Junyu Xuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文审查了六种KV缓存压缩方法在查询无关（agnostic）协议下的表现，发现查询可见性显著改变方法排名：仅KeyDiff持续优于简单基线，而广泛部署的SnapKV甚至不如“保留开头和最近窗口”策略。各方法性能下降幅度与其评分信号对查询的可见程度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩方法多在查询感知（query-aware）协议下评估，即压缩前已知查询。但实际部署中为复用压缩缓存，压缩必须在查询到来前完成（查询无关协议）。本文旨在对比这两种协议下的方法表现，揭示查询可见性对排名的影响。

Method: 在三个开源7-9B模型上，对六种已发布压缩方法与三个简单基线进行匹配预算审计。固定模型、压缩比、实例、解码等所有因素，仅改变评分规则。在RULER-8192上进行144,300次配对评估，LongBench上40,800次，并用50,000次重采样配对自助法估计不确定性。

Result: 1) 查询可见性改变排名：在无关协议下，五种共享注意力后端的方法中，仅KeyDiff在36个单元格中31个持续击败最佳三选一基线；广泛部署的SnapKV平均落后于“保留开头和最近窗口”策略（-0.066）。2) 两协议间的性能下降幅度与各方法评分信号对查询的可见度一致：SnapKV下降最大（Δ=+0.198，查询在其64-token观察窗内），KeyDiff最小（Δ=+0.011，评分不含查询项）。

Conclusion: 查询无关压缩是实际部署的关键场景，但当前主流方法在该协议下表现不佳。KeyDiff因其评分不依赖查询，在无关协议下表现最稳健。未来研究应优先开发真正查询无关的压缩方法，而非仅在查询感知设置下优化。

Abstract: KV-cache compression methods are predominantly evaluated with the query appended to the context before compression -- a query-aware protocol. Yet the economic case for a compressed KV cache is reuse: compress a document once, answer many future questions against it. In that deployment, compression must happen query-agnostic -- before any question is seen. We present a matched-budget audit of six published compression methods against three trivial baselines on three open 7-9B models (144,300 paired evaluations on RULER-8192; 40,800 on LongBench; 50,000-resample paired bootstrap throughout). Everything is held fixed -- model, compression ratio, instances, decoding -- except the scoring rule. Three findings. (1) Query visibility changes the rankings: under the agnostic protocol, of the five audited methods that share a common attention backend, only KeyDiff beats a best-of-3 trivial baseline consistently (31 of 36 cells), and the most widely deployed method, SnapKV, loses to "keep the start and the recent window" on average (-0.066). (2) The per-method drop between the two protocols is ordered consistently with how visible the question is to each method's scoring signal, legible in its source code: from Delta=+0.198 for SnapKV (the question sits inside its 64-token observation window) down to Delta=+0.011 for KeyDiff (its score contains no query term at all).

</details>


### [51] [Graph-Constrained Policy Learning for Extreme Clinical Code Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11954)
*Amritpal Singh,Sebastian Torres,Khawar Shakeel,Syed Ahmad Chan Bukhari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图约束的遍历策略，将ICD-10-CM编码预测建模为在剪枝后的编码层级上进行有限视界决策过程，单一语言模型逐层下降选择有效子节点直到到达可计费的叶子编码，将极端多标签预测转化为稀疏、层级感知的子集决策，保证结构有效输出。在MIMIC-IV数据集上，最佳监督策略SFT-1+在50码子集上达到0.709 micro-F1，在完整15,761码空间上达到0.527 micro-F1，显著优于扁平基线（CAML、LAAT、PLM-ICD），尤其在宏F1上提升0.157，缓解了罕见编码瓶颈。消融实验表明：单一共享策略匹配三专家级联模型且避免了28-32%的上下文窗口溢出；增加监督轨迹数据持续提升性能，而GRPO强化学习在同等数据下无增益。结论：简单的图约束策略学习优于更复杂的扁平、级联和强化学习替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床编码预测面临大规模、稀疏且深度层级化的标签空间（ICD-10-CM叶子编码）。现有系统多将其视为扁平多标签分类，独立为每个编码评分，导致罕见编码训练信号不足，且忽略层级结构约束，输出可能不符合编码规范。需要一种能利用层级结构、保证输出有效性、并缓解长尾分布问题的方法。

Method: 提出图约束遍历策略：将ICD预测建模为在剪枝后的编码层级图上进行有限视界决策过程。单一语言模型从根节点开始，逐层选择有效子节点，直到到达可计费的叶子编码。该方法将极端多标签预测分解为一系列稀疏、层级感知的子集决策，天然保证输出的结构有效性。训练采用监督微调（SFT）生成轨迹数据，并对比了GRPO强化学习。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV出院摘要上：SFT-1+在精选50码子集达到0.709 micro-F1，在完整15,761码空间达到0.527 micro-F1，优于扁平基线（CAML、LAAT、PLM-ICD）。完整设置下比最强扁平基线提升0.044 micro-F1和0.157 macro-F1，表明图约束分解缓解了罕见编码瓶颈。消融研究显示：单一共享策略性能匹配三专家级联模型，且避免了28-32%测试样本的上下文窗口溢出；增加监督轨迹数据唯一持续提升性能的干预；GRPO强化学习在同等数据下不优于监督续写。

Conclusion: 简单的图约束策略学习能有效解决极端临床编码预测问题，通过层级分解将复杂多标签任务转化为序列决策，显著提升罕见编码性能，且避免了级联模型的上下文溢出问题。监督轨迹数据规模是关键因素，强化学习在当前设定下无额外收益。该方法为大规模层级标签空间的结构化预测提供了可扩展、高效的范式。

Abstract: Clinical code prediction maps unstructured discharge summaries to ICD-10-CM leaf codes in a large, sparse, and deeply hierarchical label space. Most systems treat the task as flat multi-label classification, scoring codes independently and providing limited training signal for rare labels. We propose a graph-constrained traversal policy that formulates ICD prediction as a finite-horizon decision process over a pruned code hierarchy. A single language model descends the graph level by level, selecting valid child nodes until billable leaf codes are reached. This converts extreme multi-label prediction into sparse, hierarchy-aware subset decisions while guaranteeing structurally valid outputs.
  On MIMIC-IV discharge summaries, our best supervised policy, SFT-1+, achieves 0.709 micro-F1 on a curated 50-code subset and 0.527 micro-F1 on the full 15,761-code space, outperforming flat baselines including CAML, LAAT, and PLM-ICD. In the full setting, SFT-1+ improves over the strongest flat baseline by 0.044 micro-F1 and 0.157 macro-F1, suggesting that graph-constrained decomposition mitigates the rare-code bottleneck. A controlled factorial study evaluates architecture, training algorithm, and data budget. Across both scales, one shared policy matches a three-specialist cascade while avoiding its context-window overflow on 28-32% of full-space test notes. Increasing supervised trajectory data is the only intervention that consistently improves performance, while GRPO reinforcement learning provides no benefit over supervised continuation with matched data. These results show that simple graph-constrained policy learning can outperform more complex flat, cascaded, and reinforcement-learning alternatives for extreme clinical code prediction.

</details>


### [52] [BattVAE-GP: Generative Modeling of Long-Horizon Battery Degradation with Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11943)
*Raghvender Raghvender,Mahdi Abid,Ferran Brosa Planella,Charles Delacourt,Arnaud Demortière*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一个用于锂离子电池退化轨迹代理建模的混合物理-概率学习框架（BattVAE-GP），结合变分自编码器（VAE）和稀疏多任务高斯过程（GP），实现长时程退化建模的高效、不确定性感知预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理基模拟虽提供机理洞见但计算昂贵，限制了在长循环寿命中对操作条件的密集探索，需要一种计算高效且能量化不确定性的代理模型。

Method: 1) 用PyBaMM的DFN/P2D模型生成循环级退化数据；2) 转换为容量对齐的电压及导数特征，经VAE编码为二维潜在空间；3) 在潜在空间用循环数和C-rate训练稀疏多任务GP，实现连续插值和后验不确定性估计；4) 通过冻结的VAE解码器还原电压-容量演化，经蒙特卡洛传播至辅助的潜在空间到SOH预测器，得不确定性感知的SOH估计。

Result: 协议级留出评估显示潜在空间GP能准确恢复未见C-rate轨迹，不确定性行为与训练数据支持一致；在未见内部C-rate下，生成的潜在轨迹合理位于相邻模拟协议之间；解码后得到平滑电压-容量演化，蒙特卡洛传播给出不确定性感知的SOH估计。

Conclusion: BattVAE-GP框架提供了计算高效、不确定性感知的长时程退化代理模型，为电池健康预测向更丰富操作条件扩展及未来仿真-实验融合奠定结构化基础。

Abstract: Long-horizon physics-based simulations of battery degradation provide mechanistic insight but remain computationally expensive, limiting their use for dense exploration of operating conditions over extended cycle life. Here, we propose a hybrid physics-probabilistic learning framework for surrogate modeling of lithium-ion battery degradation trajectories at unseen charging rates. Cycle-resolved degradation data generated with a DFN/P2D electrochemical model in PyBaMM are first transformed into capacity-aligned voltage and derivative features and encoded using a Variational Autoencoder (VAE). The resulting two-dimensional latent space organizes degradation trajectories according to both cycle progression and charging protocol. A sparse multitask Gaussian process (GP) is then trained in this latent space using cycle number and C-rate as input variables, providing continuous interpolation of latent degradation dynamics together with posterior uncertainty estimates. Under protocol-level holdout evaluation, the latent-space GP accurately recovers unseen C-rate trajectories and exhibits uncertainty behavior consistent with the support of the training data. When queried at unseen interior C-rates, the model generates latent trajectories that remain coherently positioned between neighboring simulated protocols. Decoding the GP-predicted latent states through the frozen VAE decoder yields smooth voltage-capacity evolution, while Monte Carlo propagation of the GP latent posterior through an auxiliary latent to State of Health (SOH) predictor provides uncertainty-aware SOH estimates. The proposed BattVAE-GP framework therefore offers a computationally efficient and uncertainty-aware surrogate for long-horizon degradation modeling, providing a structured basis for extending battery health prediction toward richer operating conditions and future simulation-experiment fusion.

</details>

*Yushi Hirose,Hiroo Irobe,Takafumi Kanamori*

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种广义框架，通过线性组合成分风险构建无偏风险估计器，涵盖了PNU学习并扩展到多类分类，推导了最小可达方差，证明在非对称损失场景下比PNU学习方差更低，并建立了将方差降低与学习性能提升直接关联的泛化界，据此设计了两种实用半监督学习方法，在二分类和多分类基准上达到或优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 典型半监督学习方法依赖分布假设，假设被违反时性能下降；PNU学习虽提供无分布假设的替代方案，但仅限二分类且方差最优性不明确，亟需一种推广到多类分类且具有理论保证的框架。

Method: 提出广义框架，利用成分风险的线性组合构造无偏风险估计器，涵盖PNU学习并扩展至多类分类；推导最小可达方差，证明在非对称损失下估计器方差低于PNU；建立将方差降低与学习性能提升直接关联的泛化界；基于理论洞察设计两种实用半监督学习方法。

Result: 理论上证明了估计器可达到比PNU学习更低的方差（非对称损失场景），并建立了方差降低与泛化性能提升的直接联系；实验上，两种提出的方法在二分类和多分类基准上经验性地匹配或优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该广义框架解决了PNU学习仅限二分类且方差最优性不明的问题，通过最小方差推导和泛化界建立，为半监督学习提供了理论支撑更强且适用范围更广的无偏风险估计方法，实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Typical semi-supervised learning (SSL) methods rely on distributional assumptions, and their performance degrades when these are violated. While PNU learning, a risk rewriting method, offers a distribution-free alternative, it is restricted to binary classification and its variance optimality remains unclear. In this paper, we propose a generalized framework that constructs unbiased risk estimators using linear combinations of component risks, subsuming PNU learning and extending to multiclass classification. We derive the minimum achievable variance, demonstrating our estimator can attain lower variance than PNU in asymmetric loss scenarios. Furthermore, we establish a generalization bound directly linking this variance reduction to improved learning performance. Based on these theoretical insights, we introduce two practical SSL methods that empirically match or outperform existing approaches on binary and multiclass benchmarks.

</details>


### [54] [Scale-Aware Attention for Scarce Neural Data: An RG-Flow Transformer on Sleep-EDF EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11950)
*Dibakar Sigdel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 RG-Flow Transformer，引入重整化群归纳偏置处理 EEG 睡眠分期，但在有限数据下性能与普通 Transformer 无显著差异，普通 Transformer 甚至略优；RG-Flow 的优势在于可解释性，能恢复频谱指数 β。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑电场位呈无标度特性，功率谱遵循 1/f^β 规律，β 反映皮层状态和睡眠深度。现有 Transformer 缺乏显式尺度感知归纳偏置，难以利用该物理先验。作者旨在验证引入重整化群（RG）机制的 Transformer 是否能在真实、稀缺 EEG 数据上超越普通 Transformer，并能否学习到与频谱指数 β 对应的异常维数 γ。

Method: 构建 RG-Flow Transformer，包含普通自注意力、尺度感知流（可学习异常维数 γ）、块自旋粗粒化、熵门控同步桥接。在 PhysioNet Sleep-EDF 数据集上，采用严格无泄漏的受试者级留一交叉验证，与参数量匹配的普通 Transformer 和仅含层次结构的消融模型对比，评估 5 分类 AASM 睡眠分期；扫描每受试者数据预算以寻找归纳偏置交叉点；检验 RG-Flow 学到的 γ 是否能域外恢复实测频谱指数 β。

Result: 在 5 受试者、5 种子下，RG-Flow 与普通 Transformer 5 分类准确率无显著差异（77.3% vs 77.0%，配对 p=0.294）；预测的稀缺数据交叉点未出现，普通 Transformer 在所有受限数据预算下数值上略优；但 RG-Flow 能域外恢复连续频谱指数（β 恢复 R²=0.416），普通 Transformer 无此能力。

Conclusion: 引入显式 RG 归纳偏置的 Transformer 在小样本 EEG 睡眠分期任务上未表现出性能优势，普通 Transformer 甚至略好；但 RG-Flow 提供了可解释性增益，能学习与物理频谱指数 β 对应的表示，这为神经生理信号建模中的物理先验融合提供了新视角。

Abstract: Brain field potentials are scale-free: their power spectra follow a $1/f^β$ law whose aperiodic exponent $β$ tracks cortical state, and sleep depth in particular is a shift in $β$. We ask whether a transformer endowed with an explicit renormalization-group (RG) inductive bias -- the RG-Flow Transformer, which couples ordinary self-attention to a scale-aware stream with a learnable anomalous dimension $γ$, block-spin coarse-graining, and an entropy-gated synchronization bridge -- has an advantage over a parameter-matched vanilla transformer on \emph{real, scarce} EEG. Using the PhysioNet Sleep-EDF corpus with a strict leakage-free by-subject hold-out, we (i) benchmark RG-Flow against a param-matched vanilla transformer and a hierarchy-only ablation on 5-class AASM sleep staging, (ii) sweep the per-subject data budget to look for the inductive-bias crossover predicted when data are scarce, and (iii) test whether RG-Flow's learned $γ$ tracks the measured spectral exponent $β$ out-of-sample -- a quantity the vanilla model does not possess. Across $5$ subjects and $5$ seeds under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation, RG-Flow and the vanilla transformer are statistically indistinguishable on 5-class staging (77.3\% vs 77.0\% accuracy; paired $p=0.294$), and the predicted scarce-data crossover does not appear: vanilla is numerically ahead at every data-limited budget. What does separate the models is interpretability -- RG-Flow recovers the continuous spectral exponent out-of-sample ($β$-recovery $R^2 = 0.416$), a capability the vanilla architecture has no analogue for.

</details>


### [55] [Scalable Optimal Transport Algorithm for Network Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11952)
*Elaheh Hassani,Durga Mandarapu,Qi Yu,Hanghang Tong,Ariful Azad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FastAlign 是一种可扩展的、稀疏感知的最优传输网络对齐框架，在保持对齐精度的同时，通过稀疏感知图计算和内核融合（包括自定义 SpMM 内核），在 CPU 上实现 3.89x-9.45x 加速，在 GPU 上实现 2.24x-32.54x 加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的网络对齐方法通过反复构建和更新稠密矩阵获得高精度，但牺牲了可扩展性。需要解决这一可扩展性瓶颈，同时不降低对齐精度。

Method: FastAlign 保留原始最优传输（OT）公式，将其计算重新解释为一组反复出现的混合稀疏-稠密运算。结合稀疏感知图计算与领域特定的内核融合，包含自定义的稀疏矩阵-稠密矩阵乘法（SpMM）内核。

Result: FastAlign 实现了与最先进的基于 OT 的方法相当的对齐质量，同时在 CPU 上将端到端运行时间减少 3.89x-9.45 倍，在 GPU 上减少 2.24x-32.54 倍。

Conclusion: FastAlign 通过稀疏感知计算和内核融合，成功在不牺牲精度的前提下大幅提升了基于最优传输的网络对齐的可扩展性，为大规模网络对齐提供了实用的高性能解决方案。

Abstract: Network alignment identifies node correspondences across different networks and is a fundamental primitive in many data science applications, including social network analysis, fraud detection, and knowledge graph integration. However, state-of-the-art network alignment methods often achieve high accuracy by repeatedly constructing and updating dense matrices, sacrificing scalability in the process. To address this scalability limitation without compromising alignment accuracy, we present FastAlign, a scalable, sparsity-aware framework for optimal transport-based network alignment. Rather than introducing a new alignment model, FastAlign preserves the original OT formulation and reinterprets its computation as a set of recurring mixed sparse-dense operations. FastAlign combines sparsity-aware graph computation with domain-specific kernel fusion, including a custom SpMM kernel. Our results show that FastAlign achieves alignment quality comparable to state-of-the-art OT-based methods while substantially reducing end-to-end runtime up to 3.89x-9.45x on CPU and 2.24x-32.54x on GPU.

</details>


### [56] [When Does Reward Teach State? A Hidden-Automaton Instrument and the Group-Language Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11953)
*Jim Allchin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种白盒工具，将任务建模为隐确定性有限自动机（DFA），精确测量强化学习智能体的奖励获取与潜在状态表示学习之间的关系。研究发现：高奖励并不代表任务理解；优化器强度、任务结构、观测信息量三个可控轴决定二者的耦合；感知缺口（状态不可线性恢复但可表示）与规划缺口（状态可恢复但未被使用）可被区分；潜在状态是否可被恢复可在训练前预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习评估中，高奖励常被视为任务理解的证据，但“真实状态”通常未定义，无法判断智能体是学到了潜在状态还是仅利用奖励相关的捷径。作者旨在构建一个可精确量化潜在状态表示的测试床，分离奖励成功与状态学习，并揭示二者耦合的机制。

Method: 将任务表达为隐确定性有限自动机（DFA），智能体观测符号流并间歇性选择下一符号（部分控制），仅在接受时给予稀疏终端奖励。利用已知自动机获得最优回报（归一化分数）和每步精确潜在状态，无需展示即可探测智能体表示。通过三个可控轴（优化器强度、任务结构、观测信息量）系统研究奖励获取与状态学习的耦合。

Result: 弱在线策略RL下智能体获奖但状态探测处于随机水平；PPO+GAE可部分恢复状态但种子方差高。任务结构中的置换（群语言）结构可从转移函数预先计算，在153个新自动机上以0.86精度标记感知缺口（单向）。无标签辅助任务在观测无状态信息时无效，随观测揭示状态程度成比例恢复。成功区分感知缺口（状态不可线性恢复但可表示）与规划缺口（状态可恢复但未使用）。高奖励非任务理解证据；潜在状态可恢复性可预测。

Conclusion: 强化学习智能体的高奖励不等于任务理解；潜在状态表示的学习受优化器、任务结构、观测信息量共同制约。通过白盒DFA测试床可将奖励成功与状态学习解耦，识别感知缺口与规划缺口，并实现训练前对状态可恢复性的预测。这为评估智能体真实理解能力提供了严格基准。

Abstract: Does a reinforcement-learning agent that earns high reward represent its task's latent state, or only a reward-correlated shortcut? The question is usually unanswerable: the "true state" is undefined. We make it exactly answerable with a white-box instrument: express the task as a hidden deterministic finite automaton (DFA), let the agent observe a symbol stream and intermittently choose the next symbol under partial control, and grant one sparse terminal reward for acceptance. Knowing the automaton gives two things for free: the optimal return (so reward becomes an interpretable normalized score) and the exact latent state at every step (so we can probe the agent's representation without ever showing it). Reward success and latent-state learning thus become separately measured quantities whose coupling is governed by three controllable axes. Optimizer strength: under weak on-policy RL the agent earns reward with the state probe at chance for every architecture, tempting the conclusion that sparse RL cannot install latent state; a pre-registered control overturns it -- PPO+GAE recovers the state, but only partially and with high seed variance. Task structure: permutation (group-language) structure is a warning sign computable from the transition function before any training, and held out on 153 capacity-controlled fresh automata it flags perception gaps at precision 0.86 (89 of 103), in one direction only. Observation informativeness: a label-free auxiliary is vacuous when observations carry no state and recovers it in proportion to how much they reveal. The payoff is a distinction reward-only evaluation cannot make: a perception gap (latent state not linearly recoverable, though representable) versus a planning gap (state recoverable but unused). High reward is thus not evidence of task understanding; whether an agent recovers latent state is predictable in advance.

</details>


### [57] [Exact and Certified Data Shapley for Weighted k-Nearest-Neighbor Regression and Soft-Label Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11956)
*Zongye Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出加权KNN回归Data Shapley的伪多项式时间精确算法（基于计数动态规划），并给出针对连续权重和目标的认证FPTAS、复杂度下界、软标签多类扩展，开源库与精确基准值；实验显示精确值与蒙特卡洛Data Shapley在误标检测上统计等价，但蒙特卡洛无法在任何预算下复现精确前10%排名，精确性的价值在于确定性、认证误差界及可审计基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Data Shapley精确算法仅支持无权重KNN和加权KNN分类，加权KNN回归和软标签预测因归一化分母破坏了多项式算法依赖的加法/阈值/重复结构，仅能用O(N^K)暴力枚举，急需填补这一空白。

Method: (i) 基于联合整数状态（权重和w、加权目标和w*y）的计数动态规划，实现伪多项式时间精确计算；(ii) 针对连续权重和目标，设计认证FPTAS并提供逐值误差证书；(iii) 建立无条件Omega(D_w)输出规模下界与访问模型难度结果；(iv) 推广至加权软标签多分类。

Result: 在12,716个对抗实例上与穷举枚举零差异验证；86,400次误差证书检查无违例；下游误标检测中精确值与蒙特卡洛Data Shapley数据集级TOST检验统计等价（n=8, p<10^-4），但蒙特卡洛在高达3000次排列（~128万效用评估）预算下均无法复现精确前10%排名。

Conclusion: 精确加权KNN回归Data Shapley已实现，其核心价值不在于提升下游任务统计功效，而在于提供确定性、可认证误差界、可作为审计估计器的精确基准，填补了理论与工具链的关键空白。

Abstract: Data Shapley is the standard principled answer to which training points are worth what, and its k-nearest-neighbor (KNN) specialization is the version deployed in practice: the exact estimator shipped by toolkits such as pyDVL and OpenDataVal. Exact algorithms are known for unweighted KNN and for weighted KNN classification, but weighted KNN regression and soft-label prediction have resisted: the only exact method is an O(N^K) brute force, exponential in neighborhood size K. The obstruction: the weighted regression prediction is a ratio of two coalition-dependent sums, whose normalization denominator breaks the additive, threshold, and duplication structures the prior polynomial algorithms rely on. We close this gap. We give (i) the first pseudo-polynomial-time exact algorithm (polynomial in N and K at fixed lattice precision) for weighted KNN-regression Data Shapley, a counting dynamic program over the joint integer state (sum of w, sum of w*y), verified against exhaustive enumeration with zero mismatch on 12,716 adversarial instances; (ii) a certified FPTAS for continuous weights and targets, with a machine-checkable per-value error certificate never violated across 86,400 checks; (iii) a complexity landscape, including an unconditional Omega(D_w) output-size lower bound and access-model hardness results; and (iv) a weighted soft-label multi-class extension. We release an open-source, CPU-only library and the first exact weighted-regression Data Shapley ground truth. On downstream mislabel detection our exact values are statistically equivalent to Monte-Carlo Data Shapley (dataset-level TOST, n=8, p<10^-4), the pre-registered outcome; the value of exactness is instead determinism, a certified error bound, and an exact reference for auditing estimators: Monte-Carlo did not reproduce the exact top-10% ranking at any budget tested, up to 3,000 permutations (~1.28e6 utility evaluations).

</details>


### [58] [Constructed Reality, Contested Priors: Decoupling and the Architecture of Cognitive Relapse Under the Free Energy Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11958)
*MD Ibrahim Hossain Ridoy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过卷积变分自编码器与循环潜在预测器的计算代理，研究预测系统在自由能原理下的“本体论倒置”现象，发现表征能力与默认行为解耦，中间排练比率下出现“认知复发”，证明抗拒现实采纳是结构性属性而非学习速度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究预测系统能否被合成环境永久取代其原先生成模型（本体论倒置），但直接在神经系统中诱导和监测该转换在伦理和技术上不可行，因此通过计算代理研究其底层计算问题。

Method: 使用卷积变分自编码器配对循环潜在预测器，其证据下界目标在数学上等同于变分自由能。网络先在基线视觉域训练，再在混合流中训练，通过排练比率 r 控制基线内容在向目标域过渡时的保留程度。分别追踪表征能力（潜在空间可辨别性）和默认行为（无约束时的生成输出）。

Result: 在 90 次全参数扫描中，表征准确率维持在 0.97-0.998 的高位，与 r 无关；而默认行为随 r 变化跨越系统几乎整个范围，二者显著解耦。在中间 r 值下，默认输出先向目标域靠近，随后在训练持续时部分回退至基线，呈现“认知复发”结构性失效模式。

Conclusion: 对现实采纳的抗拒并非可归因于学习速度，而是具有独特失效模式的结构性属性，本文仅作为计算存在性证明确立了这一点。

Abstract: Under the free energy principle, a predictive system does not observe reality directly; it maintains a generative model of the world and experiences that model's best current hypothesis. Can a synthetic environment be made consistent enough that a predictive system's own inference machinery adopts it as this default hypothesis, permanently displacing the environment that first shaped it? We call this state ontological inversion. Because inducing and monitoring such a transition in a nervous system is neither ethical nor technically feasible, we study the underlying computational problem through a controlled proxy: a convolutional variational autoencoder paired with a recurrent latent predictor, whose evidence lower bound objective is mathematically identical, up to sign, to variational free energy itself. The network is trained first on a baseline visual domain, then on a mixed stream in which a swept rehearsal ratio r controls how much baseline content persists during transition to a target domain. Representational capacity, what the latent space can discriminate, is tracked separately from default behavior, what the system generates when left unconstrained. Across a full sweep of 90 runs, the two diverge sharply: representational accuracy stays near ceiling, 0.97 to 0.998, regardless of r, while default behavior spans nearly the system's entire range depending on r alone, a decoupling of learning from acceptance. More strikingly, at intermediate r the system's default output rises toward the target domain, then partially reverts toward the baseline while training continues unchanged, a structural failure we term cognitive relapse. Resistance to reality-adoption is not reducible to learning speed; it is a structural property with its own distinct failure modes, established here as a computational existence proof and nothing further.

</details>


### [59] [Evaluating Reliability in Machine Learning Models for Early Chronic Kidney Disease Prediction: A Systematic Review of Data Leakage and Predictor Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11963)
*Mashrul Hossain,Nafesa Kibria,Fahim Shahriar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过系统文献综述评估了19项慢性肾脏病预测研究，引入信息泄露分类和量化评分框架，发现高泄露研究报告准确率显著虚高（95.48% vs 80.2%），且超过80%的预测特征缺乏跨研究稳定性，揭示了许多报告的性能提升源于方法学缺陷而非真实预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对慢性肾脏病早期检测中机器学习研究存在的不一致性和潜在误导性，特别是缺乏对方法学问题（如数据泄露、时间序列记录获取受限、临床指标报告不一致）的系统性评估，本研究旨在通过系统文献综述和结构化泄露评估框架，量化方法学缺陷对模型性能的影响，并检验预测特征的可重复性。

Method: 采用系统文献综述方法，从主要学术数据库中筛选了19项相关CKD预测研究。构建了结构化信息泄露分类体系和量化泄露评分框架，对各研究的方法学可靠性进行系统评估。同时开展跨研究特征稳定性分析，考察预测变量的一致性与可重复性。

Result: 高泄露研究报告的平均准确率为95.48%，而无泄露研究仅为80.2%，性能虚高约15.28%。跨研究特征稳定性分析显示，仅有极少数预测因子具备一致的可重复性，超过80%的特征缺乏可靠性。

Conclusion: 许多CKD预测研究报告的性能提升主要源于方法学局限（尤其是信息泄露）而非真实的预测能力。现有文献中的预测特征普遍缺乏跨研究稳定性，提示当前研究成果的临床转化价值有限，未来研究需严格控制方法学偏倚并加强特征选择的可重复性验证。

Abstract: The early detection of Chronic Kidney Disease using machine learning has attracted significant interest in healthcare-related computer science. Despite rapid advancements in this field, many reported studies remain inconsistent and potentially misleading. A significant drawback is the lack of organized evaluation regarding methodological concerns. Key issues include data leakage, limited access to temporal patient records and inconsistency in reported clinical indicators. This research offers a systematic literature review of existing CKD prediction studies using interpretable machine learning techniques, where nineteen relevant studies were selected via systematic searches across major academic databases. To assess methodological reliability, this study introduces a structured taxonomy of information leakage and a quantitative leakage scoring framework to systematically evaluate reliability across CKD prediction studies. The analysis reveals a strong relationship between leakage and inflated performance. Here, High leakage-studies report an average accuracy of 95.48%, compared to 80.2% for leakage-free studies, reflecting an increase of approximately 15.28%. Furthermore, a cross-study feature stability analysis shows that only a small subset of predictors is consistently reproducible, with over 80% lacking reliability. Overall, the findings suggest that many reported performance improvements stem from methodological limitations rather than true predictive capability.

</details>


### [60] [LIDAR-AD: A Decoder-Free Latent-Interaction Dreamer with Action-Residual Chains for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11964)
*Yongzhi Liu,Yang Xiao,Zhong Cao,Zeng Kang,Sunan Zhang,Zhaozhi Dong,Guojun Yu,Weichao Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LIDAR-AD 是一种无解码器的潜在交互 Dreamer，通过动作残差链实现自动驾驶的长时程闭环决策，用潜在对齐替代观测重建，建模残差动作更新，提升风险感知状态抽象、连续控制建模和长时程动力学预测，在仿真和真实场景中优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要在动态交通环境中进行长时程闭环决策。潜在世界模型虽提供基于想象的决策框架，但多源观测包含控制无关冗余，而可靠驾驶决策依赖风险相关关系、未来动态和连续动作调整。这种不匹配使得观测重建和绝对动作建模在学习决策相关潜在动力学时表现次优。

Method: 提出 LIDAR-AD，一种无解码器的潜在交互 Dreamer 与动作残差链。1) 用冗余减少的潜在对齐替代观测重建，鼓励多源驾驶输入中风险相关关系的紧凑表示。2) 将车辆控制建模为残差动作更新，并使用残差动作序列对比学习对齐多步残差驱动的展开与未来潜在状态。3) 确定性分析表明潜在-tanh残差参数化在保持内部动作可达性的同时，将平滑长时程控制表示为紧凑的局部更新。

Result: 在多样化的仿真驾驶场景中进行广泛实验，LIDAR-AD 持续优于世界模型基线，在基于学习的方法中获得最高奖励和最佳成功率。在基于 nuPlan 的日志重构场景上的评估进一步证明了 LIDAR-AD 在真实交通布局下的可迁移性。

Conclusion: LIDAR-AD 通过潜在对齐、残差动作建模和对比学习，有效解决了多源观测冗余与决策相关动力学学习的不匹配，显著提升了自动驾驶中的风险感知状态抽象、连续控制建模和长时程动力学预测能力，实现了最优的决策性能和真实场景迁移性。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires long-horizon closedloop decision making in dynamic traffic environments. Latent world models offer an effective framework for this problem by enabling imagination-based decision making in compact latent spaces. However, multi-source observations contain controlirrelevant redundancy, whereas reliable driving decisions rely on risk-relevant relations, future dynamics, and continuous action adjustments. This mismatch makes observation reconstruction and absolute action modeling suboptimal for learning decisionrelevant latent dynamics. We propose LIDAR-AD, a decoderfree Latent-Interaction Dreamer with Action-Residual Chains for autonomous driving. LIDAR-AD replaces observation reconstruction with redundancy-reduced latent alignment, encouraging compact representations of risk-relevant relations in multi-source driving inputs. It further models vehicle control as residual action updates and uses residual-action sequence contrastive learning to align multi-step residual-driven rollouts with future latent states. A deterministic analysis shows that the latent-tanh residual parameterization preserves interior action reachability while representing smooth long-horizon control as compact local updates. Together, these designs improve risk-aware state abstraction, continuous-control modeling, and long-horizon dynamics prediction. Extensive experiments across diverse simulated driving scenarios demonstrate that LIDAR-AD consistently outperforms world-model baselines, achieving the highest reward and the best success rate among learning-based methods. Evaluations on nuPlan-derived log-reconstructed scenarios further demonstrate the transferability of LIDAR-AD under real-world traffic layouts.

</details>


### [61] [Beyond Coordinate Gauge: An Audited Protocol for Detecting Donor-Specific Functional Fingerprints after Neural Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11967)
*Truong Xuan Khanh,Phan Thanh Duc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用五个独立训练的MNIST神经网络，通过仿射校正对齐将供体头部映射到受体坐标，证明神经崩溃后供体特定的功能指纹仍可被检测，所有20个有序供体-受体对均被正确识别（p=0.0083），且通过泄露审计验证；但仅确立了可检测性，未涉及可移植性或因果持久性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立训练的神经网络缺乏共享神经元索引参考系，比较需考虑坐标自由度；神经崩溃现象使网络收敛至共享低维几何，引发问题：收敛后轨迹特定的功能变异是否仍可区分。研究旨在解决三个主张中的第一个：可检测性。

Method: 使用五个独立训练的MNIST网络重建神经崩溃，应用经验证的仿射校正对齐映射将供体头部映射到受体坐标，进行受体级基线校正，通过排列检验评估供体-受体对识别准确性，并进行泄露审计以确保鲁棒性。

Result: 所有20个有序供体-受体对均被正确识别，排列检验p值为0.0083，结果对泄露审计具有鲁棒性，表明供体特定的功能指纹在神经崩溃后仍可被检测。

Conclusion: 研究确立了在受控实验设置下的可检测性，但未证明可移植性或因果持久性；展示了对齐、歧义诊断和泄露控制相结合如何测试跨网络变异，但该结果能否推广至其他设置仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Independently trained neural networks have no shared neuron-index reference frame, so comparing them requires accounting for coordinate freedom. Neural Collapse sharpens this problem: networks converge toward a shared, low-dimensional geometry, raising the question of whether trajectory-specific functional variation remains distinguishable after convergence. We distinguish three claims - detectability, transplantability, and causal persistence - and address the first. Using five independently trained networks reconstructing Neural Collapse on MNIST, we apply a verified affine-correct alignment mapping donor heads into recipient coordinates. Donor-specific functional fingerprints remain distinguishable after recipient-level baseline correction: all 20 ordered donor-recipient pairs are correctly identified, with an exact permutation p=0.0083, robust to a leakage audit. These findings establish detectability under the test used here, but not transplantability or causal persistence. The study shows how alignment, ambiguity diagnostics, and leakage control combine to test cross-network variation in a controlled setting; whether this generalizes beyond it is open.

</details>


### [62] [Self-Evolving In-Context Learning for Direct Pilot-to-Beamformer Design in MU-MISO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11970)
*Yubo Zhang,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种增强的上下文学习（ICL）框架，用于改进多用户多入单出（MU-MISO）系统中的导频波束成形性能。通过整合ICL-Transformer主干与导频编码器-解码器网络和波束成形编码器-解码器网络，结合课程学习、自演化机制和失配感知扩展，实现无需重新训练即可适应多种信道模型，并在导频设置下持续优于WMMSE基准和近期Transformer方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导频波束成形方法在多用户MISO系统中难以同时处理多种信道模型且往往需要重新训练，同时面临收敛性、鲁棒性不足以及信道失配等挑战，亟需一种能够快速适应多样信道环境且无需显式校准的通用框架。

Method: 1. 构建增强ICL框架：集成ICL-Transformer主干、导频编码器-解码器网络（EDN）和波束成形EDN。2. 建立模型特定上下文数据集，使网络能处理多种信道模型而无需重新训练。3. 引入三大创新：(a) 课程学习策略，从监督LMMSE标签模仿平滑过渡到无监督和率最大化；(b) 自演化机制，在课程学习训练期间动态扩展和细化所有信道模型的上下文数据集；(c) 失配感知扩展，将多种失配纳入ICL框架，绕过显式信道校准。

Result: 消融研究验证了上下文架构和增强训练策略的有效性。仿真结果显示，该方案能在多样通信环境中快速适应已见和未见信道模型，无需基于梯度的参数更新，并能通过智能上下文构建缓解失配问题。在导频设置下，该方案持续优于WMMSE基准和近期基于Transformer的方法。

Conclusion: 提出的增强ICL框架通过上下文学习实现了灵活、鲁棒且高性能的导频波束成形，为多用户MISO系统提供了无需重新训练即可适应多样信道环境的解决方案，展示了在实际部署中的应用潜力。

Abstract: We develop an enhanced in-context learning (ICL) framework to improve the performance of pilot-based beamforming in multi-user multiple-input single-output (MU-MISO) systems. The proposed scheme integrates the ICL-Transformer backbone with the pilot encoder-decoder network (EDN) and the beamformer EDN. A crucial feature of our ICL network is that it can handle multiple channel models without retraining, enabled by the construction of model-specific context datasets. To improve convergence and robustness, we introduce three key innovations: (a) a curriculum learning (CL) strategy that smoothly transitions from supervised LMMSE-labeled imitation to unsupervised sum-rate maximization, (b) a self-evolving mechanism that dynamically expands and refines the context datasets for all channel models during CL-based training, and (c) a mismatch-aware extension that incorporates several mismatches into the general ICL framework and bypasses explicit channel calibrations. Ablation studies validate the effectiveness of the in-context architecture and enhanced training strategies. Simulation results over diverse communication environments show that the proposed scheme is able to rapidly adapt to both seen and unseen channel models without gradient-based parameter updates, and can mitigate the mismatch issues via intelligent context constructions. Furthermore, our scheme consistently outperforms the existing beamforming schemes under pilot-based settings, including the WMMSE benchmark and the recent Transformer-based methods.

</details>


### [63] [Learning to Discretize: Diffusion-Based Adaptive Mesh with Spectral Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11974)
*Zixuan Shen,Bingchuan Wang,Zhi Wang,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出基于扩散模型的两阶段框架学习自适应离散化，将网格生成视为物理约束的条件生成问题，在五种PDE机制中表现优于基线，证明离散化应依据问题机制学习而非固定启发式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经PDE代理模型在固定网格上学习场演化，但网格选择决定了建模能力在空间、分辨率和频谱带宽上的分配，这一隐含设计选择应当可学习，即代理模型应能在预测场演化前学会分辨率应存在的位置。

Method: 提出两阶段扩散框架：第一阶段学习条件于观测动力学的r自适应位移网格，第二阶段从网格感知表示预测解演化。网格生成器通过物理感知代理通道、几何有效性约束和局部频谱集中度正则化，保证自适应在物理上可解释且数值合法。

Result: 在五种PDE机制下实验，基于扩散的学习离散化与自适应网格和降阶基线具有竞争力，在固定或手工分配不足的机制中增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: 主要结论不是存在通用最优网格规则，而是离散化应以机制依赖方式学习：不同空间和频谱结构偏好不同分配行为。这将神经PDE求解器的自适应网格从求解器专用启发式重构为生成式表征学习问题。

Abstract: Most neural partial differential equation (PDE) surrogates learn how fields evolve after a grid has already been chosen. However, before any operator is applied, the grid has already determined how modeling capacity is allocated across space, resolution, and spectral bandwidth. We argue that this hidden design choice should itself be learnable, leading to a question different from standard operator learning: can a surrogate learn where resolution should exist before predicting field evolution? We formulate adaptive discretization as a physics-constrained conditional generation problem over valid mesh displacements. The success of diffusion models in PDE field prediction suggests their potential for learning adaptive discretizations under similar structured constraints. This leads to a two-stage diffusion framework: Stage 1 learns an r-adaptive displacement mesh conditioned on the observed dynamics, while Stage 2 predicts the solution evolution from the mesh-informed representation. The mesh generator is regularized by physics-aware proxy channels, geometric validity constraints, and local spectral concentration so that adaptation remains physically interpretable and numerically legal. Across five PDE regimes, the results show that diffusion-based learned discretization is competitive with adaptive-mesh and reduced-order baselines, with particularly strong gains in regimes where fixed or handcrafted allocation is insufficient. The main conclusion is not that there exists a universal optimal mesh rule, but that discretization should be learned in a regime-dependent manner: different spatial and spectral structures favor different allocation behaviors. This reframes adaptive meshing for neural PDE solvers from a solver-specific heuristic into a generative representation-learning problem.

</details>


### [64] [Signal-Guided Optimization for Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11975)
*Xujia Li,Dan Li,Jian Lou,Wenjie Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GSUO是一种引导信号感知的遗忘优化框架，通过任务特定的细粒度引导信号解决现有遗忘方法过度遗忘和遗忘不足的问题，在随机子集和类别级遗忘任务上均优于14个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘方法主要依赖全局粗粒度干预策略，缺乏精确的引导信号和可微分指导。由于训练样本记忆强度差异，统一策略导致部分样本过度遗忘损害模型效用，部分样本遗忘不足留下可被隐私攻击利用的残留信息。

Method: 提出GSUO框架，设计任务特定的细粒度引导信号来引导遗忘过程，适用于随机子集遗忘和类别级遗忘任务。通过引导信号感知的优化机制，实现精确、可微分的遗忘控制。

Result: 大量实验表明，GSUO在遗忘有效性和泛化能力上均优于14个基线方法，同时实现了高效率和显著加速，验证了其在可靠机器遗忘方面的有效性。

Conclusion: GSUO通过引入任务特定的细粒度引导信号，有效解决了现有遗忘方法过度遗忘和遗忘不足的问题，为机器遗忘提供了更精确、高效且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Current machine unlearning methods predominantly rely on global, coarse-grained intervention strategies. They lack precise pilot signals to guide the unlearning process and fail to provide differentiable guidance across different unlearning tasks. Due to the varying memorization strengths of samples during original training, such a uniform strategy leads to two problems: some samples are over-unlearned, which harms model utility; while others are under-unlearned, leaving residual information that can be exploited by privacy attacks. In this paper, we propose GSUO, a guidance-signal-aware unlearning optimization framework that designs task-specific fine-grained guidance signals to steer the unlearning process and is applicable to both random-subset and class-wise forgetting tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GSUO outperforms 14 baselines in terms of both unlearning effectiveness and generalization, while achieving high efficiency and significant speedups, validating its effectiveness for reliable machine unlearning.

</details>


### [65] [LiteTopK: Exploiting the Curse of Dimensionality for a Fused Indexer-TopK Kernel in Long-Context Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11976)
*Ziqi Yin,Jianyang Gao,Peiqi Yin,Jiangneng Li,Gao Cong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LITETOPK是一种高效融合的Indexer-TopK内核，利用高维空间距离集中现象，通过采样估计分数范围并在线分桶，减少全局内存流量、同步开销和内存占用，同时保持精确Top-k正确性，在GLM 5.2预填充阶段实现1.2倍加速且内存开销更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU-based Indexer-TopK内核（如DeepSeek Sparse Attention）存在过多全局内存流量、昂贵同步和过高内存开销等问题，导致效率低下。

Method: 利用高维空间中向量距离集中于狭窄范围的“维度灾难”现象，先采样小部分数据估计查询-数据分数范围，再根据估计将候选结果在线划分为多个桶，维持紧致近似阈值，仅回写有希望的候选，减少不必要I/O，大幅降低内存开销，同时保证精确Top-k正确性。

Result: 实验表明LITETOPK在真实部署场景下将GLM 5.2的预填充阶段加速1.2倍，且内存开销更低。

Conclusion: LITETOPK通过利用高维空间特性设计的在线分桶策略，有效解决了现有Indexer-TopK内核的效率瓶颈，在保持精确性的同时显著提升性能并降低内存占用，适用于大语言模型稀疏注意力和向量检索等场景。

Abstract: Indexer-TopK, the operation to compute the scores and select the top-k candidates, is widely used by sparse attention kernels in large language models and vector retrieval in recommendation systems and vector databases. However, existing GPU-based Indexer-TopK kernels like DeepSeek Sparse Attention (DSA) remain inefficient due to excessive global memory traffic, costly synchronization, and prohibitive memory overhead. In this work, we exploit the curse of dimensionality in high-dimensional spaces, where distances between high-dimensional vectors tend to concentrate within a narrow range, to design LITETOPK, a novel and efficient fused Indexer-TopK kernel. LITETOPK first samples a small subset of data to estimate query-data score ranges, then uses these estimates to partition candidate results into bins online. This organization allows the LITETOPK kernel to maintain a tight approximate threshold, write back only promising candidates, reduce unnecessary I/O, substantially lower memory overhead, and still preserve exact Top-k correctness. Experimental results show that LITETOPK accelerates the prefill stage of GLM 5.2 by 1.2x in real-world deployment scenarios while incurring lower memory overhead.

</details>


### [66] [Gene Expression-Informed Jointly Controlled Generative Modeling for Precision Molecular Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11978)
*Hang Yuan,Chen Li,Wenjun Ma,Tadahiko Murata,Yuncheng Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出JoPMol，一种融合基因表达谱生物学状态、文本分子结构和数值化学性质的联合控制精准分子生成模型，实现多条件协同下的药物分子生成与优化，在多项指标上超越现有方法，并展现出强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准分子设计需要同时控制生物学相关性（反映疾病/扰动下的细胞功能状态）和分子设计策略（结构意图与性质优化），现有方法难以在统一框架下联合建模这两类异构条件。

Method: 构建JoPMol模型，将基因表达谱编码的生物学状态、基于文本的分子结构信息、数值化的化学性质三者纳入统一建模框架，通过联合条件控制实现候选分子的协同生成与优化。

Result: 实验表明JoPMol在多个评价指标上优于最先进方法；在迁移任务和生物学仿真场景中均表现出强泛化能力，验证了其在精准分子设计中的有效性。

Conclusion: JoPMol通过整合多模态条件实现了精准分子的联合生成与优化，为个性化药物发现提供了有效工具，代码已开源。

Abstract: Precision molecular design aims to discover personalized drug candidates through joint control of multiple conditions, such as biological relevance and molecular design strategies. Biological relevance reflects cellular functional states under disease or perturbation conditions, while molecular design strategies provide complementary guidance in terms of structural intentions and property optimization. In this study, we propose JoPMol, a jointly controlled precision molecular generative model that integrates biological states encoded by gene expression profiles with molecular structure information expressed in text, and chemical properties quantified by numerical values within a unified modeling framework. This formulation enables coordinated generation and optimization of candidate molecules under joint condition control. Experimental results show that JoPMol outperforms state-of-the-art methods across multiple evaluation metrics. Moreover, JoPMol demonstrates strong generalization ability in both transfer tasks and biologically grounded simulation scenarios, validating its effectiveness for precision molecular design. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/hala-yh/JoPMol.

</details>


### [67] [Sparse Inter-Layer Dependencies of Transformer FFN Neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11990)
*Johannes Knittel,Hanspeter Pfister*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Transformer中前馈网络（FFN）块的神经元级稀疏依赖关系，提出无需训练的归因方法，发现少量上游神经元激活和注意力输出即可高保真保留目标神经元激活，同时应用神经元级掩码在中等稀疏度下基本不损失困惑度，揭示FFN虽参数稠密但存在结构化稀疏依赖，为电路级可解释性和高效推理提供工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FFN块占Transformer参数和计算大头，但残差流的加性叠加导致其内部结构难以解释。研究动机是探究FFN神经元激活是否可由稀疏的前序神经元激活和注意力输出解释，从而揭示层间稀疏依赖结构。

Method: 提出免训练归因方法，估计上游神经元和注意力输出对目标神经元激活的相对影响；将其余输入用平均值掩码替换，跨模型和层实证测试少量前序激活保留目标激活的保真度；同时在所有层应用神经元级掩码，观察偏差传播对困惑度的影响。

Result: 跨模型和层，极少量前序激活和注意力输出即可高保真保留神经元激活；考虑上游层固有激活稀疏性后有效稀疏度更高；全层同时应用神经元级掩码使偏差传播时，中等稀疏度下模型困惑度基本不变。

Conclusion: 尽管参数稠密，FFN在神经元层面展现稀疏且结构化的层间依赖。该方法为电路级可解释性提供实用可扩展工具，识别出的稀疏路径对高效推理有潜在启示。

Abstract: Feedforward network (FFN) blocks account for a large fraction of the parameters and computation in Transformer architectures, yet their internal structure remains difficult to interpret due to the additive superposition induced by the residual stream. We examine whether the activation of an FFN neuron can be explained by a sparse set of preceding neuron activations and attention outputs. We introduce a training-free attribution method that estimates the relative influence of upstream neurons and attention outputs on a target neuron's activation. Empirically, across models and layers, we find that small subsets of preceding activations and attention outputs suffice to preserve neuron activations with high fidelity when all remaining inputs are masked with their average values. Effective sparsity is even greater when accounting for the inherent activation sparsity of upstream layers. Moreover, applying the neuron-specific masks in all layers simultaneously, such that the induced deviations propagate through the network, leaves model perplexity largely unchanged at moderate sparsity levels. These results demonstrate that, despite dense parameterization, FFNs exhibit sparse and structured inter-layer dependencies at the neuron level. Our method provides a practical, scalable tool for circuit-level interpretability and identifies candidate sparse pathways with potential implications for efficient inference.

</details>


### [68] [Mitigating The Effect of Class Imbalance in Data with Hierarchical and Dependable Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11994)
*Bipin Chhetri,Deepika Giri,Avishek Kadel,Rabin Kumar Karki,Akbar Siami Namin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种层次感知的 RoBERTa 框架，通过可学习的父类嵌入显式融合 CWE 结构信息，解决漏洞分类中的极端类别不平衡和层级依赖问题，实验表明该方法优于过采样技术，在无需数据增强的情况下达到 0.76 加权 F1，显著提升少数类表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CWE 漏洞分类面临极端类别不平衡和强层级依赖，现有过采样技术（如 SMOTE、ADASYN）在层级文本分类中的有效性未被探索，且高维嵌入空间的合成插值会破坏父子约束，对深度学习模型甚至有害。

Method: 提出层次感知 RoBERTa 框架，引入可学习的父类嵌入显式编码 CWE 分类法结构，保持分类一致性，避免合成样本破坏层级约束。

Result: 在 CWE Research Concept 数据集上，无需数据增强即实现 0.76 加权 F1，超越所有基线；少数类显著提升，如 Class 类别 F1 从 0.40 提升至 0.60（相对 BERT 基线）。

Conclusion: 层次感知表示学习比过采样更适合结构化漏洞分类，能有效利用分类法结构，避免合成数据破坏层级约束。

Abstract: Classifying cybersecurity vulnerabilities using the Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) taxonomy is challenging due to extreme class imbalance and strong hierarchical dependencies among weakness categories. Although oversampling techniques such as Synthetic Minority Oversampling Technique (SMOTE) and Adaptive Synthetic Sampling (ADASYN) are widely adopted to mitigate class imbalance, their effectiveness for hierarchical CWE text classification remains largely unexplored. This paper proposes a Hierarchy-Aware RoBERTa framework that explicitly incorporates CWE structural information through learnable parent-class embeddings, preserving taxonomic consistency. Our experiments demonstrate that synthetic interpolation in high-dimensional embedding spaces violates the inherent parent-child constraints of the CWE hierarchy, offering only marginal benefits for classical ML models while consistently degrading deep learning architectures. Evaluated on a CWE Research Concept dataset, the proposed model achieves a weighted F1-score of 0.76 without data augmentation, outperforming all baselines with notable gains on minority classes, including the Class category whose F1-score improved from 0.40 to 0.60 over the BERT baseline. Our results suggest that hierarchy-aware representation learning is a more principled alternative to oversampling for structured vulnerability classification.

</details>


### [69] [Are we Merging the Right Models? Impact of Expert Training Duration on Model Merging for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11997)
*Nikita Kozodoi,Zainab Afolabi,Jack Butler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现模型合并性能与专家模型训练时长强相关，简单平均法在过拟合时急剧下降，稀疏化方法在验证损失最优点之后仍能提升，建议联合选择训练时长与合并方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多任务模型合并惯例是在专家模型达到最优验证损失时进行合并，但缺乏对训练时长对合并质量影响的系统性研究。

Method: 在五个领域（数学、代码、指令遵循、多语言、安全）对三种规模的Qwen模型（0.8B、2B、4B）进行微调，保存从最优步数25%到500%的检查点，并在每个时长评估五种合并方法。

Result: 简单平均法随过拟合急剧退化，而稀疏化合并方法在验证最优点之后仍达到最佳性能。通过偏差-方差分解分析，类比随机森林，高方差个体学习者使平均受益。

Conclusion: 训练时长与合并方法应联合选择而非独立决定，过度训练的专家模型配合适当合并方法可提升多任务性能。

Abstract: Multi-task model merging combines separately trained expert models into a single model that handles all tasks without co-training. Standard practice merges experts at their optimal validation loss.
