The framework employs four specialized LLM components in sequence: a Generator that produces diverse analytic geometry problems, a Formalizer that converts each problem into CDL for SDF-based rendering, a Measurer that extracts ground-truth answers through vision-based measurement on the rendered diagrams, and a Quality Verifier that checks outputs at three stages. Structured feedback from the Quality Verifier drives automatic retry, forming a closed loop that eliminates any need for human annotation. Applying FormalAnalyticGeo at scale yields AnalyticGeo7K, a dataset of over 7K verified multimodal problems, each with aligned text, diagram, formal annotation, and ground truth.Experiments show that the generated problems achieve a median ground-truth relative error of 0.70\%, with 82.3\% of answers falling within 5\% of the exact symbolic solution. Our framework and dataset will be publicly released.

</details>


### [170] [Resist and Update: Counterfactual Report Coordinates for Incentive-Compatible LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12985)
*Sen Yang,Yuen-Hei Yeung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出激活层面的反事实报告坐标（CRC）钳制方法，解决语言模型在激励压力下的错误报告，实现对禁用影响的抵抗与对合法证据的响应双重目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐语言模型在非证据性激励压力（用户自信、声望、文风重写）下会错误报告，表现为内部激励相容性（IC）失效，需建立因果契约使报告对禁用影响不变、对合法证据响应。

Method: 在贝叶斯见证者基准上，利用互换干预因果识别低秩报告坐标（答案、置信度、警示），提出无需训练的反事实报告坐标（CRC）钳制，引用模型在反事实激励中性上下文中的报告。

Result: 双通道钳制在见证者基准同时达抵抗与更新1.00（95% CI [0.99,1.00]），因果认证；全局解码与转向显示单参数权衡；微调仅同时枚举双目标时匹配；单通道部署版有损（0.73/0.97）；跨三模型家族复现并迁移至SycophancyEval。

Conclusion: 贡献在于接口与认证方法：激活层面的反事实激励不变性作为内部激励相容性的结构原语。

Abstract: Aligned language models routinely misreport under non-evidential incentive pressure: they agree with a confident user or overstate certainty even when their internal belief is unchanged. We cast this as a failure of internal incentive-compatibility (IC) and present a method for learning and certifying counterfactual report mediators that hold a model's reports to a causal contract: invariant to forbidden influences (pressure, prestige, restyling) and responsive to licensed ones (genuine evidence). These two demands, resist and update, pull in opposite directions. We study them on a Bayesian-witness benchmark with known posteriors, in which the same user disagreement is licensed evidence or forbidden pressure purely by stated source reliability. We (i) causally identify, by interchange interventions rather than probe accuracy, low-rank report coordinates for answer, confidence, and caveat that are near-orthogonal and independently controllable, and (ii) introduce a training-free counterfactual report-coordinate (CRC) clamp that references the model's own report under a counterfactually incentive-neutralized context. On the witness benchmark the two-pass clamp attains resist and update of 1.00 jointly (Wilson 95% CI [0.99,1.00]), a causal certificate under a constructible reference, not a deployed solution. Global decoding and steering show a single-parameter tradeoff; output-level fine-tuning matches both objectives only when both are enumerated; resist-only training loses evidence-responsiveness. The deployable single-pass compilation is lossy (0.73/0.97). The mechanism and clamp reproduce across three model families and transfer to a natural sycophancy benchmark (SycophancyEval). Our contribution is the interface and certification method: activation-level counterfactual incentive-invariance as a structural primitive for internal IC.

</details>


### [171] [Win by Silence: Deletion Non-Monotonicity, Autonomous Exploitation, and Typed-State Gating in LLM Plan Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12986)
*Aleh Manchuliantsau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究计划评估器奖励隐性计划的问题，提出了一种分阶段期望值评分器，并发现通过删除内部转换可以提高评分。实验显示所有路由至少有一个提分删除，优化器找到击败基线的结构。GATE拒绝发布沉默路由的评分，修订后修复为覆盖结构。PCSC检测并中和事后遗漏拼接。GATE作为确定性搜索塑形约束，而非仅事后过滤器，不验证语义完整性或现实质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计划评估器可能因遗漏必要工作而给予更高评分，导致遗漏激励。需要检测并中和这种评估漏洞。

Method: 提出分阶段期望值评分器，推导评分变化公式（Proposition 1），在冻结的26条路由队列上实验，测试删除内部转换的影响；使用评分寻求优化器重构路由；引入GATE拒绝发布沉默路由评分；PCSC检测遗漏拼接；自适应编译器感知合著者暴露注册表溯源边界。

Result: 所有57个允许删除均匹配解析恒等式和阈值符号，每条路由至少有一个提分删除；优化器在21/26路由中找到击败基线的未覆盖结构；GATE拒绝26/26沉默路由评分，0/26诚实挂起；拒绝后47/54次修订修复为覆盖结构，严格覆盖改进从1/26升至13/26；义务通道规避保持6/6，delta索引成本底线将击败诚实路由从6/6降至3/6，沉默融资从5/6降至0/6，但未建立语义完整性。

Conclusion: 如果计划仅因遗漏必要工作而评分更高，则计划并未改进；评估创造了遗漏激励。PCSC检测并中和事后遗漏拼接。在合作设置中，GATE作为确定性搜索塑形约束，而非仅事后过滤器。它不验证任意LLM生成策略的语义完整性或现实质量。

Abstract: Plan evaluators can reward a strategic plan for becoming less explicit. This paper studies that failure in a staged expected-value scorer for LLM-generated venture routes. Proposition 1 gives the score change from deleting an interior transition while retargeting its predecessor and retaining downstream value: Delta_k = (prod_{i<k} p_i)[c_k + (1 - p_k)R_{k+1}]. On a frozen 26-route cohort, all 57 admissible deletions matched the analytic identity and threshold sign, and every route had at least one score-improving deletion. A score-seeking optimizer, allowed to restructure routes but not told the exploit mechanism, found baseline-beating uncovered structures in 21/26 routes. GATE refused score release for 26/26 silenced routes with 0/26 honest suspensions; after refusal, 47/54 next revisions repaired to a covered structure, and strict covered improvement rose from 1/26 to 13/26. An adaptive compiler-aware co-author exposed the registry-provenance boundary: obligation-channel evasions remained 6/6 across all four v1/v1.5 conditions, while delta-indexed cost floors reduced beat-honest routes from 6/6 to 3/6 and fundability-by-silence from 5/6 to 0/6 without establishing semantic completeness. If a plan scores better only because it omits necessary work, the plan did not improve; the evaluation created an omission incentive. PCSC detects and neutralizes post-hoc omission splices over model-mediated typed-state records. In the cooperative setting tested, GATE acts as a deterministic search-shaping constraint, not merely a post-hoc filter. It does not verify the semantic completeness or real-world quality of arbitrary LLM-generated strategies.

</details>


### [172] [Dynamic Resource Allocation for Ensemble Determinization MCTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13007)
*Jakub Kowalski,Adam Ciężkowski,Artur Krzyżyński,Mark H. M. Winands*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出针对集成确定化蒙特卡洛树搜索（Ensemble Determinization MCTS）的两种动态资源分配增强：动态确定化数量和动态模拟分配，在三款桌游上验证显著提升算法强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对高不确定性环境（如含随机性和隐藏信息的对抗棋类游戏），现有集成确定化MCTS在资源分配上缺乏动态自适应机制，导致搜索效率受限。

Method: 提出两大增强：1) 动态确定化数量——根据搜索过程动态增减确定化树的数量；2) 动态模拟分配——基于模拟到模拟的决策，非均匀分配模拟预算以最大化知识增益。

Result: 在Jaipur、Lost Cities、Splendor三款桌游的迭代制和时间制设置下实验，特定配置显著提升算法胜率，具有统计学显著性。

Conclusion: 动态资源分配策略能有效提升集成确定化MCTS在高不确定性游戏中的表现，为部分可观测随机博弈提供了更强的搜索框架。

Abstract: Simulation-based algorithms are especially suited for high-uncertainty environments such as adversarial board games with significant elements of randomness and hidden information. In particular, several Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) variants are commonly used in such domains. In this paper, we propose a series of enhancements for Ensemble Determinization MCTS, introducing two axes for dynamic resource allocation. First, Dynamic Number of Determinizations, increases or decreases the number of currently used determinization trees depending on the behavior of so-far search. Second, Dynamic Simulation Allocation, splits the simulation budget nonuniformly across the determinization trees, using simulation-to-simulation decisions to choose the tree with potentially the best knowledge gain. As benchmark domains, we used three popular tabletop games: Jaipur, Lost Cities, and Splendor. Testing our proposed enhancements in iteration- and time-based settings showed that particular configurations yield a statistically significant increase in the algorithm's strength.

</details>


### [173] [Audio-Native Speech Recognition with a Frozen Discrete-Diffusion Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13013)
*Harsha Vardhan Khurdula,Abhinav Kumar Singh,Yoeven D Khemlani,Vineet Agarwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用离散扩散语言模型（DiffusionGemma）替代自回归解码器进行自动语音识别，仅训练0.16%参数即可在LibriSpeech test-clean上达到6.6% WER，且仅需约8步并行解码，支持多语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ASR主要依赖自回归解码器逐token生成，作者探索离散扩散语言模型能否通过少量去噪步骤并行细化整个转录文本，实现更高效的语音识别。

Method: 为26B参数的DiffusionGemma（采用均匀随机token离散扩散而非吸收掩码方案）构建音频原生接口：冻结Whisper编码器提取声学特征，轻量投影器映射至模型嵌入空间，低秩适配器（LoRA）使冻结主干关注新模态。仅训练约42M参数（0.16%）。发现自然训练目标因梯度仅经已忽略音频的注意力到达投影器而无法对齐音频，通过冻结输出头应用CTC损失打破僵局。

Result: 在LibriSpeech test-clean上达到6.6%词错误率，无论语句长度均在约8步并行解码完成，单一适配器在六种语言上训练，本文评估了英语、印地语和普通话。

Conclusion: 离散扩散语言模型可有效替代自回归解码器用于ASR，以极少可训练参数实现并行解码，同时在多语言任务上保持具有竞争力的准确率。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition is dominated by autoregressive decoders that emit one token at a time. We ask whether a discrete diffusion language model can transcribe speech instead, refining a whole transcript in parallel over a small number of denoising steps. We train an audio-native interface for DiffusionGemma, a 26B mixture-of-experts model that generates text by uniform, random-token discrete diffusion rather than the absorbing-mask scheme common to recent diffusion language models. A frozen Whisper encoder supplies acoustic features, a lightweight projector maps them into the model embedding space, and low-rank adapters let the frozen backbone attend to the new modality.
  About 42M parameters are trained, which is 0.16 percent of the backbone. We find that the natural training objectives fail to ground the audio because their gradient reaches the projector only through attention that has already dismissed it. A connectionist temporal classification loss applied through the frozen output head breaks this deadlock. The resulting model reaches 6.6 percent word error rate on LibriSpeech test-clean, transcribes in roughly eight parallel steps regardless of utterance length, and uses a single adapter trained on six languages, which we evaluate here on English, Hindi, and Mandarin.

</details>


### [174] [Do AI Agents Know When a Task Is Simple? Toward Complexity-Aware Reasoning and Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13034)
*Junjie Yin,Xinyu Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出E3框架，通过任务感知的执行范围估计，减少LLM代理在工程任务中的冗余读取，显著降低成本、token和文件检查量，同时保持高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在自动化多步工程工作流时，往往采用最大上下文优先策略，导致过度读取已见文件和依赖，将简单编辑变成代码库审计，缺乏任务感知的执行范围估计能力。

Method: 形式化最小充分执行和代理认知冗余比率(ACRR)，提出E3(Estimate, Execute, Expand)策略：代理先估计初始操作点，执行最小可行路径，仅在验证失败时扩展范围。在MSE-Bench基准(121个编辑任务)和真实模型测试平台LLM-Case(gpt-4o编辑真实开源库)上评估。

Result: E3在MSE-Bench上达到最强基线100%成功率，成本降低85%，token减少91%，检查文件减少92%，比自适应检索基线高16%；增益在不同指令措辞和成本权重下保持。真实模型测试中，E3是最精简快速的策略，任务成功率相当，唯一短板是提供商速率限制而非错误编辑。

Conclusion: 论文将任务感知执行定位为面向工程落地的AI(EGAI)的一步，即代理的工作量锚定在任务的工程现实中。发布了框架和基准，作为执行冗余的受控探针，而非部署代理的度量。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly automate multi-step engineering and informatics workflows, yet they rarely ask how much effort a task actually requires. They often follow a maximum-context-first strategy--re-reading files and dependencies they have already seen--turning a one-line edit into a small code-base audit. We argue the missing capability is task-aware execution-scope estimation: judging a task's difficulty, the information it truly needs, and the shortest reliable path before committing budget. We formalize minimum-sufficient execution and the Agent Cognitive Redundancy Ratio (ACRR), and propose E3 (Estimate, Execute, Expand): the agent estimates an initial operating point, executes a minimum viable path, and expands scope only when verification fails. On MSE-Bench--a deterministic benchmark of 121 edits in a capability-controlled simulator--E3 matches the strongest baseline's 100% success while cutting cost by 85%, tokens by 91%, and inspected files by 92%, and further beats a strong adaptive retrieval baseline by 16%; the gains survive held-out instruction wording and essentially every cost weighting. A companion real-model harness (LLM-Case) corroborates the effect on a live gpt-4o agent editing a real open-source library, with every candidate patch graded by actually running the project's real pytest suite against a measured oracle: the over-reading is milder but real, and E3 is the leanest and fastest policy at comparable task success--its one shortfall a provider rate-limit, not a wrong edit. We frame this as a controlled probe of execution redundancy, not a measurement of any deployed agent, and position task-aware execution as a step toward engineering-grounded AI (EGAI)--agents whose effort is anchored in the engineering reality of the task. We release the framework and benchmark.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [SHEAF: Self-profiled Hardness Estimation from Answer-set Flux for Predicting Query Hardness in Graph-based ANN Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12229)
*Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新的度量指标SHEAF，用于预测图基近似最近邻搜索中每个查询所需的最小光束宽度，通过两次浅层探针搜索之间答案集合的变化来量化查询难度，在多个数据集和索引上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图基ANN搜索中光束宽度通常固定，但不同查询难度差异巨大（如SIFT1M上达到95%召回率所需光束差异超32倍）。现有代理指标局部内在维度（LID）为静态几何度量，对最小光束预测能力弱。需要一种廉价、提前、无需真值的查询级难度预测方法。

Method: 提出SHEAF（基于答案集合通量的自画像硬度估计），通过两次不同浅层探针宽度搜索中top-k答案集合的变化量表示查询硬度。设计自画像估计器将通量转化为可部署的每查询光束预测器；开发固定探针评估协议，使用观测到的最小充分光束对所有查询对各度量进行评分。

Result: 在CAGRA和HNSW等流行ANN索引及四个多样化数据集上，SHEAF在GPU和CPU上均比五种基线方法预测每查询光束更准确，保留相关性最高提升1.55倍，仅需两次浅层探针搜索且无需查询时真值。

Conclusion: SHEAF通过自画像方式利用答案集合通量动态预测查询难度，实现了高效、准确的每查询自适应光束宽度选择，显著优于现有静态几何度量，为ANN搜索系统的吞吐率与召回率权衡提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Graph-based approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search is usually governed by a beam-width parameter that trades recall for throughput and is fixed for the whole workload. Yet, queries may not be equally hard: for example, on the widely used data set SIFT1M, the beam that a query needs to reach 95\% recall varies by more than $32\times$. Therefore, serving each query at its own width would help if the system could tell, cheaply and in advance, how hard it is. The prevailing proxy for this difficulty is called local intrinsic dimensionality (LID); however, LID is static and geometric, which makes it only weakly predict the minimum beam.
  This paper presents a new measure, namely Self-profiled Hardness Estimation from Answer-set Flux (SHEAF), which represents a query's hardness as how much its own top-$k$ answer set changes between two shallow probe widths. We design a self-profiling estimator that turns this flux into a deployable per-query beam predictor; furthermore, we develop a fixed-probe evaluation protocol that scores each measure over all queries with an observed minimum sufficient beam. On popular ANN indexes such as CAGRA and HNSW across four diverse data sets, SHEAF predicts the per-query beam better than five baseline measures on both GPU and CPU by up to $1.55\times$ in held-out correlation, using only two shallow probe searches and no query-time ground truth.

</details>


### [176] [Not Only NTP: Extending Training Signal Coverage for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12277)
*Changhao Li,Shuli Wang,Junwei Yin,Senjie Kou,Yinqiu Huang,Chi Wang,Yinhua Zhu,Haitao Wang,Xingxing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出NONTP，通过TCL和TDL两个辅助目标扩展NTP的信号覆盖，解决时空局部性问题，在工业数据集和公开基准上显著提升推荐性能，且推理零开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NTP存在两个结构性训练信号局限：时域局部性（仅优化单步预测，缺乏长程行为结构监督）和空域局部性（多域序列中目标项嵌入仅从前一隐状态获取梯度，无跨域上下文显式梯度路径）。

Method: 提出NONTP，包含两个辅助目标：1) TCL（时序对比学习），采用BYOL风格EMA教师配合InfoNCE，在表示空间对齐隐状态与K步未来轨迹；2) TDL（跨域学习），均值池化跨域隐状态并通过共享预测头预测，开辟第二梯度路径且无额外参数。推理时丢弃辅助目标，零开销。

Result: 在美团四域工业数据集（全量排序）上，HR@10较NTP提升34.3%，较MBGR提升18.3%；在Amazon Movie-Book-CDs公开基准上，HR@10提升2.8%，NDCG@10提升3.7%。在线A/B测试显示CTR提升1.8%，GMV提升2.1%（p<0.01）。消融实验证实各组件独立贡献，梯度冲突作为未来工作方向。

Conclusion: NONTP通过时空双维度扩展训练信号，有效缓解NTP的局部性问题，在工业级和公开数据集上均取得显著性能提升，且推理零开销，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Next-Token Prediction (NTP) carries two structural training signal limitations. First, NTP optimizes for single-step prediction only, placing no supervised pressure on learning longer-range behavioral structure -- we term this \textbf{temporal locality}. Second, in multi-domain sequences, each target item embedding receives gradient updates exclusively from the immediately preceding hidden state, with no explicit gradient pathway from cross-domain context -- we term this \textbf{spatial locality}.
  We propose \textbf{NONTP}, extending NTP's signal coverage along both dimensions through two auxiliary objectives. \textbf{TCL (Temporal Contrastive Learning)} uses a BYOL-style EMA teacher with InfoNCE to align hidden states against a $K$-step future trajectory in representation space. \textbf{TDL (Trans-Domain Learning)} mean-pools cross-domain hidden states and predicts through the shared prediction head, opening a second gradient pathway with no additional parameters. Both are discarded at inference: zero overhead.
  On a four-domain Meituan industrial dataset (full ranking), NONTP achieves HR@10 +34.3\% over NTP and +18.3\% over MBGR. On the public Amazon Movie-Book-CDs benchmark, HR@10 +2.8\% and NDCG@10 +3.7\%. Online A/B tests confirm CTR +1.8\% and GMV +2.1\% (both $p < 0.01$). Ablation studies confirm each component contributes independently, with gradient conflict analyzed as a direction for future work.

</details>


### [177] [SlimPer: Make Personalization Model Slim and Smart](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12281)
*Siqi Wang,Xianjie Chen,Shaofeng Deng,Albert Chen,Romil Shah,Jiawei Huang,Zhaoqin Wang,Zhang Zhang,Yiqun Liu,Meilei Jiang,Anish Dubey,Moyan Mei,Tongxin Wang,Nathan Berrebbi,Misael Manjarres,Armand Sauzay,Shardul Kothapalli,Aryaman Vinchhi,Kevin Johnstone,Juheon Lee,Gufan Yin,Ziheng Huang,Justin Lin,Mert Terzihan,Yilin Qi,Cynthia Yang,Colin Peppler,Qi Ding,Ruohan Sun,Ge Song,Litao Deng,Parichay Kapoor,Matt Ma,Huihui Cheng,Jiyuan Zhang,Yanli Zhao,Yiping Han,Fangqiu Han,Ning Yao,Arun Singh,Jordan Edwards,Zhengyu Su,Abhishek Kumar,Guangdeng Liao,Ankit Asthana*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SlimPer 通过将个性化排序重构为紧凑知识库的迭代细化，解决了 Transformer 在推荐系统中计算/内存随序列长度增长的问题，实现了模型深度与用户历史长度解耦，并在 Instagram Reels 和 Feed 上显著提升用户参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统广泛采用 Transformer 架构，但其生成式预训练范式（逐 token 自回归预测）导致中间张量随序列长度线性增长，与推荐任务仅需输出单一 <user, item> 相关性分数、无 token 级监督的特性不匹配，造成算力与内存浪费。

Method: 提出 SlimPer，将个性化排序重构为对紧凑统一的 <user, item> 知识库的迭代细化。每层选择性查询原始多模态用户侧 token，计算显式相关性匹配分数并细化知识库，单层开销 O(N) 且中间表示定长。模型深度与用户历史长度解耦；引入请求级优化，跨所有候选项共享单份用户侧 token 副本进一步压缩内存。统一稀疏、稠密、序列特征于单一骨干网络，注意力机制提供内生可解释性。

Result: 在 Instagram Reels 和 Feed 部署后，用户参与度指标显著提升，系统整体流程更精简，且能有效建模 10k+ 细粒度用户历史事件。

Conclusion: SlimPer 通过知识库迭代细化机制消除了 Transformer 在推荐场景下的结构性冗余，实现了计算/内存与历史长度的解耦，统一了多模态特征并提供可解释性，在大规模工业系统中验证了其有效性与工程落地价值。

Abstract: Transformer-style architectures are increasingly adopted for industrial recommendation systems, yet they inherit a design premise misaligned with the task: generative models rely on per-token autoregressive prediction, which justifies maintaining large intermediate tensors that scale with sequence length. In contrast, recommendation systems produce a single set of relevance scores for each <user, item> pair without token-level supervision. Leveraging this observation, we propose SlimPer, which reformulates personalized ranking as iterative refinement of a compact, unified <user, item> knowledge base. At each layer, the model selectively queries raw multi-modal user-side tokens, computes explicit relevance matching scores, and refines the knowledge base, all in O(N) per-layer cost with a fixed-size intermediate representation. As a result, model depth is decoupled from user history length, enabling deeper relevance understanding without proportional growth in compute or memory; request-only optimization further trims memory by sharing a single copy of user-side tokens across all candidate items. SlimPer unifies sparse, dense, and sequence features within a single backbone and provides inherent interpretability through its attention mechanism. Deployed on Instagram Reels and Feed, SlimPer yields measurable improvements in user engagement while streamlining the overall system and enabling effective modeling of 10k+ fine-grained user history events.

</details>


### [178] [MESH: Scaling Up Retrieval with Heterogeneous Content Unification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12392)
*Jiaxing Qu,Yilin Chen,Junpeng Hou,Jinfeng Rao,Olafur Gudmundsson,Sai Xiao,Huizhong Duan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MESH统一检索扩展框架，通过模块化架构和门控偏差修正解决异构检索系统中的“异构扩展偏差”问题，实现了对新鲜和长尾内容的显著性能提升，并在Pinterest十亿级推荐系统上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模检索系统需高效覆盖多样化内容层级，但现代系统通常依赖碎片化的专用检索模型来捕捉新鲜和长尾内容。这源于一个根本挑战：异构扩展偏差，即模型容量增益在不同内容层级间不均等。

Method: 提出MESH框架，采用模块化架构集成门控偏差修正。将特征空间划分为独立领域，施加结构归纳偏差，减少稀疏项信号与高频互动特征间的干扰，保护梯度路径，改善稀疏内容的扩展行为。

Result: 新鲜项目的幂律扩展指数提升14倍。在Pinterest Related Pins线上评估中，新鲜项目重pin率提升5.5%，漏斗效率提升55%，用户留存提升0.46%。异步服务策略使系统吞吐量提升2.87倍。

Conclusion: MESH为整合碎片化检索基础设施提供了有前景的范式，能构建更具扩展性和生态感知的统一检索骨干网络。

Abstract: Optimizing large-scale retrieval hinges on the ability to efficiently surface candidates across diverse content tiers. However, to capture segments such as fresh and long-tail content, modern systems typically resort to a fragmented "zoo" of specialized retrieval models. This operational complexity is attributed to a fundamental challenge in heterogeneous retrieval systems, the Scaling Bias of Heterogeneity, where model capacity gains do not apply equally across diverse content tiers. To bridge this gap, we propose MESH as a unified retrieval scaling framework that mitigates this bias through a modularized architecture integrated with gated bias correction. By partitioning the feature space into independent domains, MESH enforces a structural inductive bias that reduces interference between sparse-item signals and high-frequency engagement features. This protected gradient path leads to improved scaling behavior for sparse content, empirically validated by a 14 times improvement in the power-law scaling exponent for fresh items. In online evaluations on Pinterest's Related Pins platform, a billion scale item-to-item recommendation system, these improvements translate into a +5.5% lift in fresh-item repins, alongside with 55% improvement in funnel efficiency and +0.46% improvement in user retention. Finally, our asynchronous serving strategy ensures production viability by delivering a 2.87 times improvement in system throughput. Our findings suggest MESH as a promising paradigm for consolidating fragmented retrieval infrastructures into more scalable and ecosystem-aware backbones.

</details>


### [179] [Where Reasoning Matters: Rethinking Latent Reasoning in Semantic ID-based Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12425)
*Shangxin Yang,Min Gao,Zongwei Wang,Junliang Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 IBA 框架，根据语义 ID 各位置的信息增益动态分配潜在推理步数，显著提升生成式推荐的准确性-计算权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义 ID 生成式推荐引入潜在推理后，固定步数分配计算资源效率低下；实证发现早期语义 ID 位置信息增益高、后期位置贡献小，因此需要按位置信息贡献动态分配有限的推理步数。

Method: 提出基于信息增益的预算分配框架 IBA，将潜在推理步数视为有限计算预算，学习跨语义 ID 位置的分配策略：高 IG 位置分配更多细化步骤，低 IG 位置分配更少，实现计算资源的自适应分配。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上，IBA 持续超越强基线，并在准确性与计算开销的权衡上优于固定步数分配或错配的启发式分配策略。

Conclusion: 按信息增益动态分配潜在推理步数能有效提升语义 ID 生成式推荐的效率与效果，IBA 为计算资源受限场景提供了实用且可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Semantic ID-based generative recommendation predicts an item by generating a short sequence of semantic ID tokens, where each token is produced autoregressively. Latent reasoning has recently been introduced to improve this process through additional hidden-state computation before each token decision. This raises a practical question: when one item is represented by a sequence of semantic ID tokens, should each token receive the same fixed number of latent refinement steps, or should these steps be allocated more effectively across positions? We study this question through position-wise information-gain (IG), which measures how much each semantic ID position reduces the uncertainty of the target item. We observe that earlier semantic ID positions usually provide higher information-gain, while later positions contribute less additional information. We further analyze that applying more refinement to high-IG positions tends to bring larger expected benefits. Based on this observation, we propose IBA, an Information-Gain Budget Allocation framework for semantic ID-based generative recommendation. IBA treats latent refinement steps as a limited computational resource and learns how to allocate them across semantic ID positions, assigning more refinement to informative positions and less to positions with smaller contribution. Experiments on multiple public datasets show that IBA consistently improves strong generative recommendation baselines and achieves a better accuracy--computation trade-off than fixed or poorly matched step allocations.

</details>


### [180] [Cheaper is Better: A Discount-Aware Network for Conversion Rate Prediction in E-commerce Recommendation System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12578)
*Ruocong Tang,Yang Huang,Xing Fang,Chenyi Yan,Chuike Sun,Jing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出折扣感知网络(DANet)，通过时频变换、分布去偏和监督回归辅助任务建模商品折扣率与转化率关系，在阿里巴巴天猫APP上部署，显著提升离线AUC和在线pCVR、GMV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后点击转化率(CVR)是在线推荐系统的关键指标，面临数据稀疏、样本选择偏差和延迟反馈等挑战。商品折扣率作为影响定价和用户购买行为的关键因素，鲜有研究关注其对CVR的影响。

Method: DANet包含三个主要组件：1) 时频变换模块，利用傅里叶变换获取频谱捕捉商品长期折扣率趋势；2) 分布去偏模块，缓解用户特定折扣率因购买组合、促销活动及周期性偏差导致的偏差；3) 监督回归辅助任务，建立显式商品折扣标签增强模型价值准确性，促进折扣率有效表征。

Result: 真实数据集实验表明DANet优于基线，离线AUC提升1.61%，在线A/B测试中pCVR提升3.63%，GMV提升2.23%，已成功部署于阿里巴巴天猫APP。

Conclusion: DANet有效建模折扣率与CVR关系，通过时频变换捕捉长期趋势、分布去偏消除偏差、辅助回归任务提升表征精度，显著改善推荐系统转化率预测性能，具有实际工业部署价值。

Abstract: Post-click conversion rate (CVR) is a crucial element in online recommendation systems, which addresses significant challenges such as data sparsity (DS), sample selection bias (SSB), and delayed feedback. However, the impact of item discount rate-a key factor influencing both pricing and user purchasing behavior, has received limited attention. In this paper, we introduce the Discount-Aware Network (DANet) to model the relationship between item discount rates and CVR. DANet comprises three main components: 1) a time-frequency transformation module that utilizes Fourier transform to derive the frequency spectrum and capture the long-term discount rate trends of items; 2) a distribution de-bias module designed to mitigate the biases in user-specific discount rates caused by various purchase combinations and promotional activities, as well as periodic deviations linked to different promotion periods on e-commerce platforms; and 3) a supervised regression auxiliary task that establishes the explicit item discount labels to enhance the model's performance in terms of value accuracy, facilitating an effective representation of item discount rates. Experimental results on real datasets demonstrate the superiority of DANet, with offline AUC improving by 1.61%, and online A/B test also shows that DANet achieves impressive gains of 3.63% on pCVR and 2.23% on GMV. DANet has been successfully deployed on Alibaba Tmall APP. The code is available at https://github.com/tangrc/DANet.

</details>


### [181] [Learning to Forget: Satiation-Aware Long-Sequence Transducers for Mitigating Post-Purchase Redundancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12714)
*Yipin Dai,Ruocong Tang,Xing Fang,Yang Huang,Jing Wang,Zhentao Song,He Guo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种饱和感知机制(SAM)用于序列推荐，显式建模用户兴趣生命周期，通过双路径交叉注意力、自适应饱和门控单元和自监督TTNP任务解决动作-意图不对称问题，将购后重复率降低60%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐模型将用户交互视为偏好积累的正向信号，但在电商场景中购买行为往往标志着特定意图的终止(Interest Exit)而非延续。现有模型忽视这一区别，导致动作-意图不对称，引发严重的购后冗余推荐。

Method: SAM包含三大核心组件：(1)双路径交叉注意力架构，回溯性抑制已满足意图的历史点击，同时从长期购买历史中检索个性化补货节奏；(2)自适应饱和门控单元(ASGU)，生成时间敏感的软掩码，购买后立即抑制已满足兴趣，随预测复购周期临近逐步重新激活；(3)自监督下次购买时间预测(TTNP)辅助任务，无需人工标注即可学习潜在商品生命周期。

Result: 在工业数据集的大量离线实验和在线A/B测试中，SAM显著将购后重复率(PPRR)降低超过60%。

Conclusion: SAM通过显式建模用户兴趣的全生命周期，有效解决了动作-意图不对称问题，在购买后即时抑制已满足兴趣并根据复购周期动态重新激活，显著减少了购后冗余推荐，提升了推荐系统的精准度和用户体验。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation models predominantly interpret user interactions as positive signals for preference accumulation. However, in e-commerce scenarios, a purchase action often signifies the termination of a specific intent ("Interest Exit") rather than its continuation. Existing models overlook this distinction, suffering from Action-Intent Asymmetry, which leads to severe post-purchase redundancy. In this paper, we propose the Satiation-Aware Mechanism (SAM), an end-to-end framework designed to explicitly model the lifecycle of user interests. SAM incorporates three key components: (1) A Dual-path Cross-Attention architecture that retroactively suppresses historical clicks associated with a fulfilled intent while simultaneously retrieving personalized replenishment rhythms from long-term purchase history; (2) An Adaptive Satiation Gating Unit (ASGU) that generates a time-sensitive soft mask to inhibit satisfied interests immediately after purchase and gradually "re-awaken" them as the predicted repurchase cycle approaches; and (3) A self-supervised Time-to-Next-Purchase (TTNP) auxiliary task to learn latent product lifecycles without manual annotation. Extensive offline experiments on industrial datasets and online A/B testing demonstrate that SAM significantly reduces the Post-Purchase Repeat Rate (PPRR) by over 60%.

</details>


### [182] [RecRec: Latent Interests Recursive Reasoning for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12945)
*Wenhao Deng,Junchen Fu,Hanwen Du,Alexandros Karatzoglou,Ioannis Arapakis,Hangjun Guo,Kaiwen Zheng,Yongxin Ni,Joemon M. Jose*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 RecRec（递归推理推荐），一种无需强化学习的框架，通过解耦推理与预测，突破了单一 d 维状态的瓶颈。包含上下文压缩器和递归推理器两阶段监督训练，支持推理深度在推理时自由调整，在四个真实数据集上超越现有推理增强方法，且在三个数据集上泛化至训练深度之外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐模型仅用单次前向传播编码用户历史，虽引入潜在推理增加推理时计算，但将推理与预测耦合在单一 d 维状态中，限制推理深度，且常依赖多阶段强化学习流程，训练复杂且难以灵活调整推理步数。

Method: RecRec 包含两个模块，分两阶段监督训练：1) 上下文压缩器将骨干网络隐藏状态蒸馏为少量潜在兴趣，配合兴趣多样性正则化促使各兴趣捕捉用户行为不同侧面；2) 递归推理器在独立中间潜在空间迭代细化这些兴趣，采用深度监督使推理深度可在推理时任意调整而无需重训练。整个框架无需强化学习。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上，RecRec 超越最先进的推理增强基线；其中三个数据集上，性能增益延伸至训练时最大推理深度之外，展现出良好的深度泛化能力。

Conclusion: 解耦推理与预测、采用多向量潜在兴趣表示的设计，有效打破了单一状态瓶颈，释放了潜在推理能力。推理状态的结构设计是序列推荐中值得进一步探索的重要设计维度。

Abstract: Sequential recommender systems rely on a single forward pass to encode user interaction histories and predict the next item. Increasing inference-time computation through latent reasoning, with the model proceeding step by step before the final prediction, has been recently explored in sequential recommendation with promising results. However, how to structure the reasoning process for sequential recommendation remains an open question. Existing approaches couple reasoning and prediction in a single $d$-dimensional state, limiting reasoning depth and often relying on multi-stage pipelines with reinforcement learning (RL). We propose RecRec (Recursive Reasoning for Recommendation), an RL-free framework that decouples reasoning from prediction, overcoming the fixed $d$-dimensional state bottleneck of prior methods. RecRec consists of a Context Compressor and a Recursive Reasoner, trained in two simple supervised stages. The Context Compressor distills the backbone's hidden states into a small set of latent interests, with an Interest Diversity Regularizer encouraging each interest to capture a distinct aspect of user behavior. The Recursive Reasoner then refines these interests by reasoning in a separate intermediate latent space. Deep supervision lets the reasoning depth be freely adjusted at inference without retraining. On four real-world datasets, RecRec outperforms state-of-the-art reasoning-enhanced methods, and on three of four datasets, gains extend past the training-time depth. Our findings point to a decoupled, multi-vector recipe that unleashes latent reasoning from the single-state bottleneck of prior methods, suggesting reasoning-state structure as a design axis to explore further in sequential recommendation.

</details>


### [183] [ViHoRec: A Quality-Controlled Vietnamese Hotel Recommendation Dataset and Cold-Start Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12946)
*Minh Hoang Nguyen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ViHoRec，一个面向越南语酒店推荐的公开数据集，包含18,267次交互，6,832用户，560家酒店，从三个平台爬取，提供可复现的构建管道、隐私保护发布和冷启动基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越南语推荐系统研究受限于缺乏公开、文档完善的酒店交互资源；构建此类资源面临跨平台实体对齐、质量审计需可复现指标、隐私保护与基准测试兼容等挑战。

Method: 从Booking.com、Traveloka、Ivivu爬取数据，设计跨平台实体消歧管道，采用定量质量控制指标审计数据质量，使用HMAC伪名化保护隐私，构建基于时间顺序的留一出冷启动分割，提供数据中心消融实验和无依赖基线模型。

Result: 在公开分割上，学习型模型对短历史用户性能大幅下降（BPR-MF Recall@10: 0.065 vs 0.120），UserKNN整体表现最强，验证了ViHoRec作为稀疏、冷启动主导的低资源推荐测试床的有效性。

Conclusion: ViHoRec填补了越南语酒店推荐数据集的空白，提供了可复现的构建流程、隐私保护发布和冷启动基准，为低资源语言推荐系统研究提供了宝贵的测试平台。

Abstract: Recommender-system research for Vietnamese remains limited by the absence of a public, well-documented hotel interaction resource. Building such a resource is challenging for three reasons: cross-platform hotel names must be reconciled before interactions are comparable; quality must be audited with reproducible metrics rather than ad hoc cleaning; and public release must preserve privacy while remaining benchmarkable under realistic cold-start conditions. We introduce ViHoRec, a quality-controlled Vietnamese hotel recommendation dataset of 18{,}267 interactions between 6{,}832 users and 560 hotels, crawled from Booking.com, Traveloka, and Ivivu. Our contributions are: (i) a reproducible construction pipeline with cross-platform entity resolution and quantitative quality control; (ii) a privacy-preserving release with HMAC pseudonyms; and (iii) a public cold-start benchmark with temporal leave-last-one-out split, data-centric ablations, and dependency-free baselines. On the public split, learned models degrade sharply for users with short histories (BPR-MF Recall@10: 0.065 vs. 0.120), while UserKNN remains strongest overall, establishing ViHoRec as a sparse, cold-start-dominated testbed for low-resource recommendation. All data are publicly available at https://github.com/MinhNguyenDS/ViHoRec.

</details>


### [184] [FAIR GraphRAG: A Retrieval-Augmented Generation Approach for Semantic Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11464)
*Marlena Flüh,Soo-Yon Kim,Carolin Victoria Schneider,Sandra Geisler*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出FAIR GraphRAG框架，将FAIR数字对象（FDO）作为图检索系统的基本节点，结合大语言模型进行模式构建和元数据自动提取，应用于胃肠病学RNA测序数据，显著提升复杂查询的问答准确性、覆盖率和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法缺乏对底层知识资源的结构化FAIR化处理，限制了其在医学等复杂领域实现FAIR信息检索的潜力。

Method: 构建FAIR GraphRAG框架：以FAIR数字对象为图节点，包含核心数据、元数据、持久标识符和语义链接；利用大语言模型支持模式构建和内容/元数据自动抽取；由医生与计算机科学家共同设计以保证临床相关性；在胃肠病学生物医学数据集（RNA测序数据）上验证。

Result: 框架在遵循FAIR原则的同时，显著提升了问答准确性、覆盖率和可解释性，特别是在涉及元数据和本体链接的复杂查询中表现优异。

Conclusion: 证明了将FAIR数据实践与基于图的检索技术相结合的可行性，并展望该方法可推广至教育、商业等其他专业领域。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) addresses the limitations of Large Language Models (LLMs) when providing responses to domain-specific questions. Graph-based RAG approaches, such as GraphRAG, enhance retrieval by capturing semantic relationships within knowledge graphs (KGs). While the FAIR principles (Findability, Accessibility, Interoperability, and Reusability) are becoming prevalent for scientific data management, especially in complex domains such as medicine, existing RAG approaches lack a structured FAIRification of the underlying knowledge resources. This lack limits their potential for FAIR information retrieval in these domains. To address this gap, we introduce FAIR GraphRAG, a novel framework that integrates FAIR Digital Objects (FDOs) as the fundamental units of a graph-based retrieval system. Each graph node represents an FDO that incorporates core data, metadata, persistent identifiers, and semantic links. We leverage LLMs to support schema construction and automated extraction of content and metadata from data sources. The framework was co-designed by physicians and computer scientists to ensure technical and clinical relevance. We apply FAIR GraphRAG to a biomedical dataset in gastroenterology, demonstrating its applicability to RNA-sequencing data. Beyond ensuring adherence to the FAIR principles, FAIR GraphRAG significantly improves question answering accuracy, coverage, and explainability, particularly for complex queries involving metadata and ontology links. This work shows the feasibility of combining FAIR data practices with graph-based retrieval techniques. We see potential for applying our approach to other specialized fields such as education and business.

</details>
